(HTMLElement.prototype as any).empty=function(){this.replaceChildren();};
// Synthetic dispatch has no physical active pointer. Event routing remains real DOM.
const nativeSetPointerCapture=Element.prototype.setPointerCapture,nativeReleasePointerCapture=Element.prototype.releasePointerCapture;
Element.prototype.setPointerCapture=function(){};Element.prototype.releasePointerCapture=function(){};
const wait=(ms=0)=>new Promise<void>(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,ms));
const frame=()=>new Promise<void>(resolve=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>resolve()));
function assert(value:any,message:string){if(!value)throw new Error(message);}
async function until(test:()=>any,message:string){const deadline=performance.now()+4000;while(!test()){if(performance.now()>deadline)throw new Error(message);await wait(10);}}
function button(root:ParentNode,label:string):HTMLButtonElement{
  let node=[...root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('button')].find(item=>item.getAttribute('aria-label')===label||item.textContent===label);
  if(!node&&label==='固定视图'){button(root,'学习集更多设置').click();node=button(root,'固定视图…');}
  const labels:Record<string,string>={'大纲':'大纲','卡片分组看板':'卡片分组看板 ⌃⌘M','多选':'多选 ⌥⌘M','脑图手写':'脑图手写','手形工具':'手形工具'};
  if(!node&&labels[label]){
    const graph=(root as Element).matches?.('.ms-mindmap-root')?root:root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-root');
    if(graph){button(graph,'选择脑图').click();if(label==='脑图手写'||label==='手形工具')button(document.querySelector('#test-menu')!,'工具 ▸').click();node=button(document.querySelector('#test-menu')!,labels[label]);}
  }
  assert(node,'Missing button '+label);return node!;
}

const select=(node:HTMLElement,key:string)=>node.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown',{key,bubbles:true,cancelable:true}));
async function closeAll(){for(const p of active.splice(0)){await p.view.onClose();p.view.unload();p.view.containerEl.remove();await p.store.flush();}document.querySelectorAll('.ms-modal-bg,#test-menu,.test-host-floating-ai').forEach(node=>node.remove());target.replaceChildren();}
async function test(name:string,action:()=>any){try{await closeAll();await action();results.push({name,passed:true});}catch(error){results.push({name,passed:false,error:String(error),stack:(error as any).stack});}}

async function fresh(saved?:any,hostOptions:{leftCollapsed?:boolean;rightCollapsed?:boolean;background?:boolean}={}){
  let disk:any={};const sources=new Map<string,{file:TFile;content:string;binary?:ArrayBuffer;delay?:number}>(),writes=new Map<string,string>(),folders=new Set<string>();
  const add=(path:string,content:string,binary?:any,delay=0)=>{const file=new TFile(path,content.length);sources.set(path,{file,content,binary:binary?new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(binary)).buffer:undefined,delay});return file;};
  const filler=Array.from({length:65},(_,index)=>`<p>Paragraph ${index}: source context supports reading-position restoration.</p>`).join('');
  const alpha=add('Alpha.html','<p>Alpha anchor target.</p>'+filler),beta=add('Beta.md','# Beta document\n'+Array.from({length:50},(_,i)=>`Beta paragraph ${i}.`).join('\n'));
  const gamma=add('Gamma.docx','',{content:'<p>Gamma converted document.</p>'+filler}),delta=add('Delta.pdf','',{name:'Delta',pages:3}),slow=add('Slow.html','<p>Delayed source.</p>',null,60);
  const metrics={reads:new Map<string,number>(),opens:[] as string[],layoutSaves:0,subscriptions:0,annSubscriptions:0,headerTitle:''};
  const events=new Map<string,Set<(...args:any[])=>void>>();
  const workspaceEvents=new Map<string,Set<(...args:any[])=>void>>();
  const dock=(collapsed=false)=>({collapsed,collapse(){this.collapsed=true;},expand(){this.collapsed=false;}});
  const app:any={vault:{configDir:'.obsidian',getFiles:()=>[...sources.values()].map(value=>value.file),getAbstractFileByPath:(path:string)=>sources.get(path)?.file??null,
    async read(file:TFile){const entry=sources.get(file.path)!;metrics.reads.set(file.path,(metrics.reads.get(file.path)??0)+1);await wait(entry.delay);return entry.content;},
    async readBinary(file:TFile){const entry=sources.get(file.path)!;metrics.reads.set(file.path,(metrics.reads.get(file.path)??0)+1);await wait(entry.delay);return entry.binary?.slice(0)??new TextEncoder().encode(entry.content).buffer;},
    getResourcePath:(file:TFile)=>'/synthetic-resource/'+encodeURIComponent(file.path),
    on(name:string,callback:any){const listeners=events.get(name)??new Set();listeners.add(callback);events.set(name,listeners);return{off:()=>listeners.delete(callback)};},
    adapter:{async exists(path:string){return writes.has(path)||sources.has(path)||folders.has(path);},async read(path:string){if(!writes.has(path))throw new Error('Missing synthetic adapter file '+path);return writes.get(path);},async write(path:string,value:string){writes.set(path,value);},async mkdir(path:string){folders.add(path);},async remove(path:string){writes.delete(path);},async rename(from:string,to:string){assert(writes.has(from)&&!writes.has(to),'invalid synthetic sidecar move');writes.set(to,writes.get(from)!);writes.delete(from);}}},
    metadataCache:{getFirstLinkpathDest:()=>null},workspace:{leftSplit:dock(hostOptions.leftCollapsed),rightSplit:dock(hostOptions.rightCollapsed),rootSplit:{},activeLeaf:null,centerLeaf:null,
      on(name:string,callback:any){const listeners=workspaceEvents.get(name)??new Set();listeners.add(callback);workspaceEvents.set(name,listeners);return{off:()=>listeners.delete(callback)};},offref(ref:any){ref.off();},onLayoutReady(callback:any){callback();},getMostRecentLeaf(){return this.centerLeaf;},
      requestSaveLayout(){metrics.layoutSaves++;},getLeaf(){return{setViewState:async()=>{}};},openLinkText:async()=>{},revealLeaf:async()=>{},setActiveLeaf(next:any){this.activeLeaf=next;}}};
  const store=new Store({app,loadData:async()=>structuredClone(disk),saveData:async(value:any)=>{disk=structuredClone(value);}} as any);await store.load();
  const originalSubscribe=store.subscribe.bind(store),originalAnnotations=store.subscribeAnnotations.bind(store);
  store.subscribe=(callback:any)=>{metrics.subscriptions++;const unsubscribe=originalSubscribe(callback);let done=false;return()=>{if(!done){done=true;metrics.subscriptions--;unsubscribe();}};};
  store.subscribeAnnotations=(path:string,callback:any)=>{metrics.annSubscriptions++;const unsubscribe=originalAnnotations(path,callback);let done=false;return()=>{if(!done){done=true;metrics.annSubscriptions--;unsubscribe();}};};
  store.settings.scale=.7;const set=store.createStudySet('Workspace audit',[...sources.keys()]),canvasId=store.activeCanvasId!;
  // Legacy workspace suites below target the traditional reading/writing/recall shell.
  set.scene={templateId:'standard',sceneId:'excerpt',interfaceMode:'traditional'};
  store.addCard({id:'anchor-card',docPath:alpha.path,type:'excerpt',front:'Alpha anchor',anchorAnnId:'anchor-a',color:'#ffffad',tags:['source'],links:[],createdAt:'2026-10-15T00:00:00Z'});
  store.addCard({id:'beta-card',docPath:beta.path,type:'excerpt',front:'Beta evidence',color:'#d7e8fa',tags:[],links:[],createdAt:'2026-10-15T00:00:00Z'});
  store.addNode({id:'parent',canvasId,parentId:null,title:'Study topics',x:40,y:100,collapsed:true});
  store.addNode({id:'anchor-node',cardId:'anchor-card',canvasId,parentId:'parent',title:'Alpha anchor',x:300,y:180,collapsed:false});
  store.addNode({id:'beta-node',cardId:'beta-card',canvasId,parentId:null,title:'Beta evidence',x:40,y:300,collapsed:false});
  await store.saveAnnotationsNow(alpha.path,[{id:'anchor-a',cardId:'anchor-card',docPath:alpha.path,docKind:'html',page:0,type:'highlight',rects:[],range:{start:0,end:12,exact:'Alpha anchor'},text:'Alpha anchor',color:'#ffffad',createdAt:'2026-10-15T00:00:00Z'}]);
  let view:StudyWorkspaceView;
  const plugin:any={app,store,viewEvents:{},activeReader:null,studyWorkspace:null,sidebar:{name:'independent-sidebar'},condenseService:()=>condenseServiceFor(aiServiceFor(plugin)),
    openDocument:async(file:TFile)=>{metrics.opens.push(file.path);return view.openFile(file);},refreshMindmap(){view?.refresh();},refreshSidebar(){view?.refresh();},ensureSplitLayout:async()=>{}};
  const leaf:any={app,getRoot:()=>app.workspace.rootSplit,setViewState:async()=>{},getViewState:()=>({type:'margin-study-workspace'}),updateHeader(){metrics.headerTitle=view.getDisplayText();}};
  const activate=(next:any)=>{app.workspace.activeLeaf=next;if(next?.getRoot()===app.workspace.rootSplit)app.workspace.centerLeaf=next;workspaceEvents.get('active-leaf-change')?.forEach(listener=>listener(next));};
  view=new StudyWorkspaceView(leaf,plugin);view.load();
  view.containerEl.firstElementChild!.classList.add('view-header-title');
  leaf.view=view;app.workspace.activeLeaf=app.workspace.centerLeaf=hostOptions.background?{getRoot:()=>app.workspace.rootSplit}:leaf;
  await view.setState(saved??{tabs:[{path:alpha.path}],activePath:alpha.path});
  const p={app,store,set,canvasId,plugin,leaf,view,root:view.contentEl,alpha,beta,gamma,delta,slow,sources,writes,events,workspaceEvents,activate,metrics,reloadStore:async()=>{let restoredDisk=structuredClone(disk);const restored=new Store({app,loadData:async()=>structuredClone(restoredDisk),saveData:async(value:any)=>{restoredDisk=structuredClone(value);}} as any);await restored.load();return restored;}};active.push(p);
  await view.onOpen();await frame();return p;
}

function mindmapScale(world:HTMLElement):number{
  const match=world.style.transform.match(/scale\(([^)]+)\)/);
  assert(match,'map world does not expose a rendered scale');
  const value=Number(match[1]);
  assert(Number.isFinite(value),'map world rendered scale is not finite');
  return value;
}
function ctrlPixelWheel(scroll:HTMLElement,deltaY:number,clientX:number,clientY:number,deltaX=0):WheelEvent{
  const event=new WheelEvent('wheel',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,ctrlKey:true,metaKey:false,deltaMode:0,deltaX,deltaY,clientX,clientY});
  scroll.dispatchEvent(event);
  return event;
}
function ctrlModeWheel(scroll:HTMLElement,deltaY:number,clientX:number,clientY:number,deltaMode:number):WheelEvent{
  const event=new WheelEvent('wheel',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,ctrlKey:true,metaKey:false,deltaMode,deltaX:0,deltaY,clientX,clientY});
  scroll.dispatchEvent(event);
  return event;
}
async function freshPinchScene(){
  await closeAll();
  const p=await fresh();
  p.store.addNode({id:'pinch-far-node',canvasId:p.canvasId,parentId:null,title:'Pinch far',x:1200,y:900,collapsed:false});
  p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
  const scroll=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap') as HTMLElement,world=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-world') as HTMLElement;
  const horizontalRoom=Math.max(0,scroll.scrollWidth-scroll.clientWidth),verticalRoom=Math.max(0,scroll.scrollHeight-scroll.clientHeight);
  scroll.scrollLeft=Math.min(horizontalRoom,Math.round(horizontalRoom*.2));
  scroll.scrollTop=Math.min(verticalRoom,Math.round(verticalRoom*.2));
  const rect=scroll.getBoundingClientRect(),point={x:rect.left+rect.width*.31,y:rect.top+rect.height*.37};
  return {p,scroll,world,rect,point};
}

function linkedMode(p:any,label:string){const mode=label.includes('双向')?'both':label.includes('关闭')?'off':label.startsWith('→')?'document':'mindmap';for(let i=0;i<4&&state(p).linkMode!==mode;i++)button(p.root,'设置文档与脑图联动').click();assert(state(p).linkMode===mode,'link cycle did not reach requested mode');}
function placement(p:any,label:string){const menu=layoutMenu(p);const choice=[...menu.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('button')].find(b=>b.textContent?.includes(label));assert(choice,'placement missing from workspace settings');choice!.click();}
function pointer(node:EventTarget,type:string,x:number,y:number,pointerId=7){node.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent(type,{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,pointerId,button:0,isPrimary:true,clientX:x,clientY:y}));}
function trackWindowPointerListeners(){
  const types=new Set(['pointermove','pointerup','pointercancel','blur']),live=new Map<string,Set<any>>(),nativeAdd=window.addEventListener.bind(window),nativeRemove=window.removeEventListener.bind(window);
  const mutate=(add:boolean)=>(type:string,listener:any)=>{if(!types.has(type)||!listener)return;const listeners=live.get(type)??new Set();live.set(type,listeners);add?listeners.add(listener):listeners.delete(listener);};
  window.addEventListener=((type:string,listener:any,options?:any)=>{mutate(true)(type,listener);return nativeAdd(type,listener,options);}) as typeof window.addEventListener;
  window.removeEventListener=((type:string,listener:any,options?:any)=>{mutate(false)(type,listener);return nativeRemove(type,listener,options);}) as typeof window.removeEventListener;
  return {count(type?:string){return type?(live.get(type)?.size??0):[...live.values()].reduce((sum,listeners)=>sum+listeners.size,0);},restore(){window.addEventListener=nativeAdd;window.removeEventListener=nativeRemove;}};
}
function morePane(p:any,name:string){button(p.root,'学习集更多设置').click();button(p.root.querySelector('[aria-label="StudySet - More"]')!,name).click();}
function mapInk(p:any){return p.root.querySelector('[aria-label="脑图手写画布"]') as SVGSVGElement;}

try{
  await test('A20 review context reveals the exact card in its source map and refuses another study set',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();const second=p.store.createCanvas(p.set.id,'另一个脑图');
    p.store.addNode({id:'a20-context-node',cardId:'anchor-card',canvasId:second.id,parentId:null,title:'复习来源',x:120,y:120,collapsed:false});
    assert(await p.view.revealReviewCardContext('anchor-card',second.id,p.set.id),'source map was not revealed');await frame();
    assert(p.store.activeCanvasId===second.id&&mapCard(p,'a20-context-node'),'source card not rendered in selected map');
    const other=p.store.createStudySet('其他学习集');
    assert(!await p.view.revealReviewCardContext('anchor-card',second.id,other.id),'cross-study-set map was incorrectly selected');
  });
  await test('A14 ink drawn on a card follows a 100-world-pixel drag',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();await frame();const graph=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap-root')!,node=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),world=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap-world')!,scale=mindmapScale(world),bounds=node.getBoundingClientRect();
    button(graph,'脑图手写').click();const surface=graph.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>('[aria-label="脑图手写画布"]')!;
    pointer(surface,'pointerdown',bounds.left+20*scale,bounds.top+20*scale);pointer(surface,'pointermove',bounds.left+35*scale,bounds.top+25*scale);pointer(surface,'pointerup',bounds.left+45*scale,bounds.top+30*scale);
    button(graph,'手形工具').click();await frame();const beforeNode=mapCard(p,'beta-node').getBoundingClientRect(),beforeInk=graph.querySelector<SVGGElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke]')!.getBoundingClientRect();
    pointer(mapCard(p,'beta-node'),'pointerdown',beforeNode.left+15,beforeNode.top+15);pointer(window,'pointermove',beforeNode.left+15+100*scale,beforeNode.top+15);await frame();pointer(window,'pointerup',beforeNode.left+15+100*scale,beforeNode.top+15);await frame();
    const afterNode=mapCard(p,'beta-node').getBoundingClientRect(),afterInk=graph.querySelector<SVGGElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke]')!.getBoundingClientRect(),error=Math.abs((afterInk.left-afterNode.left)-(beforeInk.left-beforeNode.left))/scale;
    assert(error<=1,'bound ink offset error '+error+' world pixels');const stroke=p.store.canvases.find((canvas:any)=>canvas.id===p.canvasId)!.handwriting!.layers[0].strokes[0] as any;assert(stroke.targetCardId==='beta-card'&&stroke.focusNodeId==='beta-node','card/node binding was not persisted');
  });
  await test('A14 attachment handwriting snapshots document ID and page',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();p.store.updateStudySet(p.set.id,{documentAttachments:{[p.alpha.path]:[p.delta.path]}});await p.view.openFile(p.delta);const reader=p.view.getReader() as ReaderView;await reader.navigateToReference(2,[],undefined,false);const sourceId=p.store.documents.find((doc:any)=>doc.path===p.delta.path)!.id;
    const graph=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap-root')!;button(graph,'脑图手写').click();const surface=graph.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>('[aria-label="脑图手写画布"]')!,bounds=surface.getBoundingClientRect();pointer(surface,'pointerdown',bounds.left+100,bounds.top+80);pointer(surface,'pointerup',bounds.left+140,bounds.top+100);
    const stroke=p.store.canvases.find((canvas:any)=>canvas.id===p.canvasId)!.handwriting!.layers[0].strokes[0] as any;assert(stroke.sourceDocPath===p.delta.path&&stroke.sourceDocId===sourceId&&stroke.sourcePage===2,'wrong writing snapshot '+JSON.stringify(stroke));
  });
  await test('A14 source jump returns to original Reader page and scroll offset',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();p.store.updateStudySet(p.set.id,{documentAttachments:{[p.alpha.path]:[p.delta.path]}});await p.view.openFile(p.delta);const reader=p.view.getReader() as ReaderView;await reader.navigateToReference(2,[],undefined,false);
    const graph=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap-root')!;button(graph,'脑图手写').click();const surface=graph.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>('[aria-label="脑图手写画布"]')!,bounds=surface.getBoundingClientRect();pointer(surface,'pointerdown',bounds.left+100,bounds.top+80);pointer(surface,'pointerup',bounds.left+140,bounds.top+100);
    await reader.navigateToReference(3,[],undefined,false);const scroll=reader.getScrollContainer();scroll.scrollTop+=35;scroll.dispatchEvent(new Event('scroll'));await frame();const before={page:reader.getCurrentPage(),top:scroll.scrollTop};
    button(graph,'套索').click();for(const [index,point] of [[60,40],[180,40],[180,140],[60,140],[60,40]].entries())pointer(surface,index===0?'pointerdown':index===4?'pointerup':'pointermove',bounds.left+point[0],bounds.top+point[1]);
    button(graph,'笔迹回源').click();await until(()=>p.view.getReader().getCurrentPage?.()===2,'source did not open snapshot page');assert(p.store.documents.find((doc:any)=>doc.path===p.view.getReader().getFilePath())?.id===p.store.documents.find((doc:any)=>doc.path===p.delta.path)?.id,'source document ID changed');
    button(p.root,'返回原阅读位置').click();await until(()=>p.view.getReader().getCurrentPage?.()===before.page,'return did not restore page');await frame();assert(Math.abs(p.view.getReader().getScrollContainer().scrollTop-before.top)<=1,'return did not restore scrollTop');
  });
  await test('A14 legacy unbound handwriting remains visible',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();p.store.updateCanvas(p.canvasId,{handwriting:{version:1,activeLayerId:'default',layers:[{id:'default',name:'Default',strokes:[{id:'a14-legacy',tool:'pen',color:'#123456',width:3,points:[{x:100,y:100},{x:150,y:120}]}]}]}});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();const stroke=p.store.canvases.find((canvas:any)=>canvas.id===p.canvasId)!.handwriting!.layers[0].strokes[0] as any;
    assert(p.root.querySelector('[data-map-ink-stroke="a14-legacy"] path')&&!('targetCardId' in stroke)&&!('sourceDocId' in stroke),'legacy ink changed or disappeared');
  });
  await test('A14 bound ink follows live movement, resize, zoom, undo and store reopen',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();await frame();const graph=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap-root')!,node=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),world=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap-world')!,scale=mindmapScale(world),bounds=node.getBoundingClientRect();
    button(graph,'脑图手写').click();const surface=graph.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>('[aria-label="脑图手写画布"]')!;pointer(surface,'pointerdown',bounds.left+25*scale,bounds.top+15*scale);pointer(surface,'pointerup',bounds.left+45*scale,bounds.top+20*scale);button(graph,'手形工具').click();await frame();
    const first=mapCard(p,'beta-node').getBoundingClientRect(),firstInk=graph.querySelector<SVGGElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke]')!.getBoundingClientRect();pointer(mapCard(p,'beta-node'),'pointerdown',first.left+10,first.top+10);pointer(window,'pointermove',first.left+10+100*scale,first.top+10+20*scale);await frame();let current=mapCard(p,'beta-node').getBoundingClientRect(),currentInk=graph.querySelector<SVGGElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke]')!.getBoundingClientRect();assert(Math.abs((currentInk.left-current.left)-(firstInk.left-first.left))/scale<=1&&Math.abs((currentInk.top-current.top)-(firstInk.top-first.top))/scale<=1,'ink did not follow while dragging');pointer(window,'pointerup',first.left+10+100*scale,first.top+10+20*scale);await frame();
    const stroke=p.store.canvases.find((canvas:any)=>canvas.id===p.canvasId)!.handwriting!.layers[0].strokes[0] as any,original=structuredClone(stroke);await p.store.flush();const reopened=await p.reloadStore(),saved=reopened.canvases.find((canvas:any)=>canvas.id===p.canvasId)!.handwriting!.layers[0].strokes[0] as any;assert(JSON.stringify(saved)===JSON.stringify(original),'binding/frame lost after reopening');
    const oldNode=p.store.getNode('beta-node')!,oldHeight=mapCard(p,'beta-node').offsetHeight;p.store.updateCard('beta-card',{front:'Much taller card content '.repeat(80)});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();const taller=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),newHeight=taller.offsetHeight;assert(newHeight>oldHeight,'fixture card did not resize');
    const path=graph.querySelector<SVGPathElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke] path')!,match=path.getAttribute('d')!.match(/^M\s+([\d.]+)\s+([\d.]+)/)!;assert(Math.abs(Number(match[1])-(oldNode.x+(original.points[0].x-original.targetFrame.x)))<=1&&Math.abs(Number(match[2])-(oldNode.y+(original.points[0].y-original.targetFrame.y)*newHeight/oldHeight))<=1,'resize did not preserve relative ink position');
    const before=graph.querySelector<SVGGElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke]')!.getBoundingClientRect(),box=taller.getBoundingClientRect(),scroll=graph.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-mindmap')!;ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,-8,box.left+20,box.top+20);await frame();const after=graph.querySelector<SVGGElement>('[data-map-ink-stroke]')!.getBoundingClientRect(),zoomed=taller.getBoundingClientRect();assert(Math.abs((before.left-box.left)/scale-(after.left-zoomed.left)/mindmapScale(world))<=1,'zoom changed relative world offset');
    assert(p.store.undoLibraryChange(),'card resize undo failed');assert(p.store.undoLibraryChange(),'node move undo failed');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();assert(p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.x===40,'undo did not restore node');assert(p.store.redoLibraryChange(),'node move redo failed');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();assert(Math.abs(p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.x-140)<=1,'redo did not restore node');

  await test('Recovery creation and cleanup do not refresh the workspace',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();await p.store.flush();await wait(150);
    let scheduled=0,renders=0;
    const view=p.view as any,oldSchedule=view.scheduleRefresh,renderer=view.renderer,oldRender=renderer.render;
    view.scheduleRefresh=function(){scheduled++;return oldSchedule.apply(this,arguments);};
    renderer.render=function(){renders++;return oldRender.apply(this,arguments);};
    const write=p.app.vault.adapter.write;
    p.app.vault.adapter.write=async(path:string,value:string)=>{const exists=await p.app.vault.adapter.exists(path);await write(path,value);if(!exists)p.events.get('create')?.forEach((listener:any)=>listener({path}));};
    try{
      p.writes.delete([...p.writes.keys()].find((path:string)=>path.startsWith('_marginstudy-recovery/pending-main-')));
      p.store.moveNode('anchor-node',333,444);await p.store.flush();await wait(150);
      assert(scheduled===0&&renders===0,`recovery scheduled ${scheduled}, rendered ${renders}`);
      p.events.get('delete')?.forEach((listener:any)=>listener({path:'_marginstudy-recovery/pending-main-device-old-abc123.json'}));
      assert(scheduled===0,'recovery deletion scheduled refresh');
      p.events.get('create')?.forEach((listener:any)=>listener({path:'ordinary.md'}));
      assert(scheduled===1,'ordinary creation did not schedule refresh');
    }finally{view.scheduleRefresh=oldSchedule;renderer.render=oldRender;}
  });
  await test('Pinch 120 frame geometry and world writes',async()=>{
    const {p,scroll,world,point}=await freshPinchScene();
    await wait(200);
    const node=mapCard(p,'beta-node');node.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click',{bubbles:true}));
    const rect=Element.prototype.getBoundingClientRect, width=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLElement.prototype,'offsetWidth')!,height=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLElement.prototype,'offsetHeight')!;
    const reads={rect:0,width:0,height:0},writes={width:0,height:0,left:0,top:0,transform:0},properties=['width','height','left','top','transform'] as const;
    const nativeRaf=window.requestAnimationFrame,frames:{reads:number;sizeWrites:number;transformWrites:number}[]=[];let measuring=false;
    const send=(target:EventTarget,type:string,id:number,x:number,y:number)=>target.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent(type,{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,pointerType:'touch',pointerId:id,clientX:x,clientY:y}));
    send(scroll,'pointerdown',301,point.x-40,point.y);send(scroll,'pointerdown',302,point.x+40,point.y);
    Element.prototype.getBoundingClientRect=function(){if(measuring)reads.rect++;return rect.call(this);};
    Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype,'offsetWidth',{...width,get(){if(measuring)reads.width++;return width.get!.call(this);}});
    Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype,'offsetHeight',{...height,get(){if(measuring)reads.height++;return height.get!.call(this);}});
    for(const name of properties)Object.defineProperty(world.style,name,{configurable:true,get(){return this.getPropertyValue(name);},set(value){if(measuring)writes[name]++;this.setProperty(name,value);}});
    try{
      for(let index=0;index<120;index++){
        window.requestAnimationFrame=((callback:FrameRequestCallback)=>nativeRaf.call(window,time=>{
          const beforeReads=reads.rect+reads.width+reads.height,beforeSizes=writes.width+writes.height+writes.left+writes.top,beforeTransforms=writes.transform;
          measuring=true;
          try{callback(time);}finally{measuring=false;frames.push({reads:reads.rect+reads.width+reads.height-beforeReads,sizeWrites:writes.width+writes.height+writes.left+writes.top-beforeSizes,transformWrites:writes.transform-beforeTransforms});}
        })) as typeof window.requestAnimationFrame;
        try{send(window,'pointermove',302,point.x+40+(index+1)*.3,point.y+(index+1)*.02);}finally{window.requestAnimationFrame=nativeRaf;}
        await frame();
      }
    }finally{
      window.requestAnimationFrame=nativeRaf;
      Element.prototype.getBoundingClientRect=rect;Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype,'offsetWidth',width);Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype,'offsetHeight',height);
      for(const name of properties)delete (world.style as any)[name];
      send(window,'pointerup',302,point.x+76,point.y+2.4);send(window,'pointerup',301,point.x-40,point.y);
    }
    const output={reads,writes,frames:frames.length,scale:mindmapScale(world),scrollLeft:scroll.scrollLeft,scrollTop:scroll.scrollTop};
    console.log('PINCH_120_RESULT '+JSON.stringify(output));
    assert(frames.length===120&&frames.every(frame=>frame.reads===0&&frame.sizeWrites===0&&frame.transformWrites===1)&&reads.rect+reads.width+reads.height<=2&&output.scale===1.45031&&output.scrollLeft===695&&output.scrollTop===633,`Pinch callbacks only; excludes reading position tracking and document-load mind map refresh: ${JSON.stringify({output,frames})}`);
  });
  await test('Colored mind map node text and menu meet contrast requirements',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),originalTheme=p.store.settings.theme,failures:string[]=[];
    const luminance=(value:string)=>{const channels=value.match(/[\d.]+/g)!.slice(0,3).map(Number).map(channel=>{const part=channel/255;return part<=.04045?part/12.92:((part+.055)/1.055)**2.4;});return .2126*channels[0]+.7152*channels[1]+.0722*channels[2];};
    const contrast=(foreground:string,background:string)=>{const values=[luminance(foreground),luminance(background)].sort((left,right)=>right-left);return (values[0]+.05)/(values[1]+.05);};
    try{
      p.store.updateNode('parent',{collapsed:false});
      for(const theme of ['light','dark']){
        p.store.settings.theme=theme;applyTheme(p.plugin);
        for(const color of [...CARD_COLORS,'#777777','#000000','#123456','#ffffff']){
          p.store.updateCard('anchor-card',{color,title:'Contrast title',back:'Contrast body'});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
          const node=mapCard(p),heading=node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-node-heading')!,menu=node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-node-menu')!,body=node.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-node-body')!;
          assert(heading&&menu&&body,`missing colored node content ${theme} ${color}`);
          const headingStyle=getComputedStyle(heading),menuStyle=getComputedStyle(menu),bodyStyle=getComputedStyle(body),background=getComputedStyle(menu.parentElement!).backgroundColor;
          const alpha=Number(menuStyle.opacity),fg=menuStyle.color.match(/[\d.]+/g)!.slice(0,3).map(Number),bg=background.match(/[\d.]+/g)!.slice(0,3).map(Number);
          const effectiveMenu=`rgb(${fg.map((channel,index)=>channel*alpha+bg[index]*(1-alpha)).join(',')})`;
          const titleRatio=contrast(headingStyle.color,headingStyle.backgroundColor),menuRatio=contrast(effectiveMenu,background),bodyRatio=contrast(bodyStyle.color,getComputedStyle(node).backgroundColor);
          if(titleRatio<4.5||menuRatio<3||bodyRatio<4.5)failures.push(`${theme} ${color}: title ${titleRatio.toFixed(2)}, body ${bodyRatio.toFixed(2)}, menu ${menuRatio.toFixed(2)}`);
        }
      }
      assert(!failures.length,failures.join('; '));
    }finally{p.store.settings.theme=originalTheme;applyTheme(p.plugin);}
  });
  await test('Dark theme renders home and workspace surfaces darker than light',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),originalTheme=p.store.settings.theme;
    const color=(node:Element|null)=>{assert(node,'missing theme surface');return getComputedStyle(node!).backgroundColor;};
    const dark=(value:string)=>{const channels=value.match(/[\d.]+/g)?.map(Number);return !!channels&&channels.length>=3&&(channels[3]??1)>=.9&&channels.slice(0,3).every(channel=>channel<100);};
    const luminance=(value:string)=>{const channels=value.match(/[\d.]+/g)!.slice(0,3).map(Number).map(channel=>{const part=channel/255;return part<=.04045?part/12.92:((part+.055)/1.055)**2.4;});return .2126*channels[0]+.7152*channels[1]+.0722*channels[2];};
    const contrast=(foreground:string,background:string)=>{const values=[luminance(foreground),luminance(background)].sort((left,right)=>right-left);return (values[0]+.05)/(values[1]+.05);};
    try {
      assert(!dark('rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)')&&!dark('rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.89)')&&dark('rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9)'),'dark alpha threshold is incorrect');
      p.store.settings.theme='light';applyTheme(p.plugin);p.view.showStudyHome();await frame();
      const home=p.root.querySelector('.ms-study-home-screen'),workspace=p.root,lightHome=color(home),lightWorkspace=color(workspace);
      p.store.settings.theme='dark';applyTheme(p.plugin);await frame();
      const darkHome=color(home),darkWorkspace=color(workspace);
      assert(dark(darkHome)&&dark(darkWorkspace)&&darkHome!==lightHome&&darkWorkspace!==lightWorkspace,`theme colors: ${lightHome}, ${darkHome}, ${lightWorkspace}, ${darkWorkspace}`);
      for(const selector of ['.ms-study-home-navigation','.ms-study-home-card','.ms-study-home-cover'])assert(dark(color(home!.querySelector(selector))),`not dark: ${selector}`);
      button(home! as ParentNode,p.set.name).click();await frame();
      for(const selector of ['.ms-workspace-toolbar','.ms-workspace-library','.ms-workspace-map','.ms-mindmap','.ms-node','.ms-workspace-reader-host','.ms-bottom-primary'])assert(dark(color(p.root.querySelector(selector))),`not dark: ${selector}`);
      const action=p.root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-action:not(.ms-bottom-scene-tab):not(:disabled)')!;
      assert(action,'missing bottom action');const pressed=action.getAttribute('aria-pressed');action.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');await wait(200);
      assert(dark(color(action)),`pressed action is not dark: ${color(action)}`);
      if(pressed===null)action.removeAttribute('aria-pressed');else action.setAttribute('aria-pressed',pressed);
      const pill=p.root.querySelector('.ms-bottom-primary');const ratio=contrast(getComputedStyle(action).color,color(pill));
      assert(ratio>=4.5,`bottom action contrast ${ratio.toFixed(2)} < 4.5`);
      button(p.root,'大纲').click();await frame();assert(dark(color(p.root.querySelector('.ms-map-floating-header'))),'outline header is not dark');
      const surface=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-study-workspace')??p.root;
      await p.view.openFile(p.delta);await until(()=>!!surface.querySelector('.ms-page'),'PDF paper missing');
      for(const [theme,paper] of [['sepia','rgb(245, 239, 223)'],['green','rgb(237, 250, 204)']]){surface.dataset.readingTheme=theme;await frame();for(const selector of ['.ms-workspace-toolbar','.ms-workspace-tabs','.ms-workspace-map','.ms-mindmap','.ms-workspace-context','.ms-bottom-primary'])assert(dark(color(surface.querySelector(selector))),`${theme} shell not dark: ${selector} ${color(surface.querySelector(selector))}`);assert(color(surface.querySelector('.ms-page'))===paper,`${theme} paper ${color(surface.querySelector('.ms-page'))} differs from ${paper}`);}
    } finally {p.store.settings.theme=originalTheme;applyTheme(p.plugin);}
  });

  await test('Interface mode command, hotkey, menu and workspace survive reload',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),plugin=new MarginStudyPlugin(p.app as any) as any;
    p.app.workspace.onLayoutReady=()=>{};
    plugin.load();await until(()=>plugin.commands.has('save-now'),'plugin commands did not load');
    try {
      plugin.store=p.store;plugin.studyWorkspace=p.view;
      const command=plugin.commands.get('toggle-interface-mode');
      assert(command&&typeof command.callback==='function','interface mode command is not registered');
      assert(command.hotkeys?.some((key:any)=>key.key==='I'&&key.modifiers.includes('Mod')&&key.modifiers.includes('Alt')),'Cmd+Alt+I hotkey missing');
      assert(queryFeatures('切换界面模式').some(entry=>entry.id==='toggle-interface-mode'),'feature search entry missing');
      p.store.setStudyInterface(p.set.id,'scenes');p.view.refresh(true);
      command.callback();await frame();
      assert(p.store.getStudyScene(p.set.id)?.interfaceMode==='traditional','command did not switch to traditional');
      assert(!p.root.querySelector('.ms-workspace-toolbar.has-scene-header'),'traditional mode retained scene header');
      await p.store.flush();
      const restored=await p.reloadStore();
      assert(restored.getStudyScene(p.set.id)?.interfaceMode==='traditional','mode did not survive store reload');
      const restoredPlugin={...p.plugin,store:restored},restoredView=new StudyWorkspaceView(p.leaf,restoredPlugin);
      restoredView.load();await restoredView.setState(state(p));await restoredView.onOpen();await frame();
      assert(!restoredView.contentEl.querySelector('.ms-workspace-toolbar.has-scene-header'),'reopened workspace did not restore traditional layout');
      await restoredView.onClose();restoredView.unload();restoredView.containerEl.remove();
      command.callback();await frame();
      assert(p.store.getStudyScene(p.set.id)?.interfaceMode==='scenes','second command did not restore scenes');
      assert(p.root.querySelector('.ms-workspace-toolbar.has-scene-header'),'scene header did not return');
      button(p.root,'学习集更多设置').click();
      button(p.root.querySelector('[aria-label="StudySet - More"]')!,'切换界面模式').click();await frame();
      assert(p.store.getStudyScene(p.set.id)?.interfaceMode==='traditional','menu did not switch mode');
    } finally {plugin.unload();}
  });
  await test('Opening a PDF through the host keeps the same tab and reaches the MarginStudy reader after the native file-open lifecycle settles',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),plugin=new MarginStudyPlugin(p.app as any) as any;
    const readers:any[]=[];
    const native=()=>({file:p.delta,getViewType:()=> 'pdf'});
    let current:any={type:'empty',state:{}};
    const leaf:any={app:p.app,view:{getViewType:()=> 'empty'},getRoot:()=>p.app.workspace.rootSplit,updateHeader(){},getViewState:()=>structuredClone(current),
      async setViewState(next:any){
        current=structuredClone(next);
        if(next.type==='pdf'){leaf.view=native();return;}
        const view=plugin.views.get(next.type)(leaf);readers.push(view);leaf.view=view;view.load();await view.onOpen();await view.setState(next.state);await wait(600);
      }};
    p.app.workspace.iterateAllLeaves=(callback:any)=>{callback(leaf);};
    p.app.workspace.getLeavesOfType=(type:string)=>leaf.view.getViewType()===type?[leaf]:[];
    p.app.workspace.onLayoutReady=()=>{};
    await plugin.onload();plugin.store.settings.replaceNativeReader=true;plugin.studyWorkspace=p.view;
    p.app.workspace.activeLeaf=leaf;
    const emit=(name:string,value?:any)=>p.workspaceEvents.get(name)?.forEach(listener=>listener(value));
    const start=notices.length;
    try{
      // Obsidian dispatches file-open before its enclosing native view operation completes.
      // That operation can still restore its PDF view after a listener returns.
      current={type:'pdf',state:{file:p.delta.path}};leaf.view=native();emit('file-open',p.delta);
      await wait(60);current={type:'pdf',state:{file:p.delta.path}};leaf.view=native();emit('layout-change');
      await until(()=>leaf.view instanceof ReaderView&&leaf.view.getFilePath()===p.delta.path,'PDF remained in the native view after file-open');
      assert(leaf.getViewState().type==='margin-study-reader','takeover created a different tab or lost reader state');
      assert(!notices.slice(start).some(text=>text.includes('阅读器加载失败')),'takeover fell back instead of completing');
    }finally{
      await wait(5500);
      plugin.onunload();plugin.unload();
      for(const listeners of [p.workspaceEvents,p.events])for(const set of listeners.values())set.clear();
      for(const reader of readers){await reader.onClose();reader.unload();reader.containerEl.remove();}
      await plugin.store.flush();
    }
  });
  await test('Missing-source scans do not interrupt the workspace and choices remain available on demand',async()=>{
    const recovered:string[]=[];

    try{
      for(let i=0;i<100;i++)prompts.show(issue);
      assert(!document.querySelector('.ms-modal-bg'),'automatic scans interrupted the workspace');
      prompts.openPending();button(document,'Removed.pdf').click();button(document,'稍后处理').click();prompts.show(issue);
      assert(!document.querySelector('.ms-modal-bg'),'Later immediately reopened the same missing source');

      prompts.show({...issue,id:'other-source',oldPath:'Other.pdf',candidates:['Replacement.pdf'],identicalCandidates:['Replacement.pdf']});
      prompts.openPending();button(document,'Other.pdf').click();
      button(document,'内容一致：Replacement.pdf').click();await wait();

      assert(!document.querySelector('.ms-modal-bg'),'restored source left a modal behind');
    }finally{prompts.dispose();}
    const nextSession=new SourceRecoveryPrompts(async()=>{},()=>{});nextSession.show(issue);
    assert(!document.querySelector('.ms-modal-bg'),'later plugin session interrupted startup');nextSession.openPending();assert(button(document,'Removed.pdf'),'manual recovery unavailable after restart');nextSession.dispose();
    assert(!document.querySelector('.ms-modal-bg'),'plugin unload leaves recovery UI behind');
  });

  await test('Selection menu preserves its captured target, stops input propagation and removes positioning listeners',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),reader=p.view.getReader() as DocumentReaderView,scroll=reader.getScrollContainer(),paragraph=scroll.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('p')[5],range=document.createRange();
    assert(paragraph?.firstChild,'text fixture missing');paragraph.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});range.selectNodeContents(paragraph);const expected=range.toString();
    const add=window.addEventListener.bind(window),remove=window.removeEventListener.bind(window),listeners=new Map<string,Set<any>>();
    window.addEventListener=((type:string,listener:any,options?:any)=>{if(type==='scroll'||type==='resize'){const set=listeners.get(type)??new Set();set.add(listener);listeners.set(type,set);}return add(type,listener,options);}) as typeof window.addEventListener;
    window.removeEventListener=((type:string,listener:any,options?:any)=>{listeners.get(type)?.delete(listener);return remove(type,listener,options);}) as typeof window.removeEventListener;
    try{
      window.getSelection()!.removeAllRanges();window.getSelection()!.addRange(range);document.dispatchEvent(new Event('selectionchange'));
      await until(()=>document.querySelector('.ms-ann-pop button'),'selection menu missing');const pop=document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-ann-pop')!;button(pop,'更多').click();const highlight=button(pop,'高亮');
      assert((listeners.get('scroll')?.size??0)>0&&(listeners.get('resize')?.size??0)>0,'positioning listeners missing');
      let leaked=0;const onClick=()=>leaked++;document.addEventListener('click',onClick);window.getSelection()!.removeAllRanges();
      highlight.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true}));highlight.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true}));highlight.click();document.removeEventListener('click',onClick);
      await until(()=>!pop.isConnected,'action did not close menu');await wait(350);assert((await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)).some((item:any)=>item.text===expected),'captured text was not committed');
      assert(leaked===0,'menu click propagated to document');assert((listeners.get('scroll')?.size??0)===0&&(listeners.get('resize')?.size??0)===0,'positioning listeners survived menu close');
    }finally{window.addEventListener=add;window.removeEventListener=remove;}
  });

    const descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator,'clipboard'),copied:string[]=[];
    Object.defineProperty(navigator,'clipboard',{configurable:true,value:{writeText:async(text:string)=>{copied.push(text);}}});
    try {
      button(p.root,'块选择：复制').click();await wait(20);
      assert(copied.length===1&&copied[0].includes('First block.')&&copied[0].includes('Middle block.')&&copied[0].includes('Last block.'),'copy lost selected block content');

      button(p.root,'块选择：复制').click();await wait(20);assert(copied.length===1,'copy accepted changed source');
    } finally {if(descriptor)Object.defineProperty(navigator,'clipboard',descriptor);else delete (navigator as any).clipboard;}
    assert(JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)})===before,'selecting range wrote objects');
  });

  });

  await test('Mixed blocks preserve repeated images, identities, order and source positions',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),dock=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="MN4 阅读工具栏"]')!,canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=20;canvas.height=20;const image=canvas.toDataURL();
    p.sources.get(p.alpha.path)!.content='<p>Before image.</p><img width="50" height="50" src="'+image+'"><p>Between images.</p><img width="50" height="50" src="'+image+'"><p>After image.</p>';await p.view.openFile(p.gamma);await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);
    const before=p.store.cards.length;button(dock,'块选择').click();await until(()=>p.root.querySelectorAll('[aria-label^="辅助摘录图片"]').length===2,'duplicate images missing');
    const frames=[...p.root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-assistive-frame')];frames[0].click();frames[frames.length-1].click();button(p.root,'块选择：更多').click();button(p.root,'块选择：文本').click();await p.store.flush();
    const card=p.store.cards[before],blocks=card.excerptBlocks,ann=(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)).find((item:any)=>item.id===card.anchorAnnId);
    assert(blocks?.length===5&&blocks.map((block:any)=>block.image?'image':'text').join(',')==='text,image,text,image,text','mixed source order lost');
    assert(new Set(blocks.map((block:any)=>block.id)).size===5&&blocks.every((block:any)=>block.target)&&JSON.stringify(ann.excerptBlocks)===JSON.stringify(blocks),'block identities or source positions lost');
    assert(blocks[1].target.range.imageIndex!==blocks[3].target.range.imageIndex&&cardImages(card).filter((value:string)=>value===image).length===2,'duplicate images collapsed');
    const node=p.store.nodes.find((item:any)=>item.cardId===card.id);await until(()=>p.root.querySelector(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"] [data-excerpt-block="${blocks[3].id}"]`),'duplicate image not displayed');const rendered=p.root.querySelector(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"]`)!;
    assert(rendered.querySelectorAll('img[data-excerpt-block]').length===2,'duplicate image display collapsed');
    p.store.updateCard(card.id,{front:card.front.replace('Before image.','Before {{c1::secret}} image.')});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const updated=p.root.querySelector(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"]`)!;
    assert(updated.querySelectorAll('img[data-excerpt-block]').length===2&&!updated.textContent?.includes('secret')&&!updated.textContent?.includes('{{c1::'),'map lost images or exposed cloze');
    const list=document.createElement('div');document.body.append(list);try{renderCardList(list,p.plugin);await wait();
    const item=list.querySelector(`[data-card-id="${card.id}"]`)!;
    assert(!item.textContent?.includes('secret')&&!item.textContent?.includes('{{c1::')&&item.querySelectorAll('img').length===2,'card list exposed cloze or lost images');
    const firstText=item.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-block="${blocks[0].id}"]`)!,firstImage=item.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-block="${blocks[1].id}"]`)!,secondText=item.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-block="${blocks[2].id}"]`)!;
    assert(!!(firstText.compareDocumentPosition(firstImage)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING)&&!!(firstImage.compareDocumentPosition(secondText)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING)&&secondText.textContent?.includes('Between images.'),'edit changed text/image order');
    const savedFront=card.front,savedBlocks=card.excerptBlocks;
    p.store.updateCard(card.id,{front:savedFront.replace('\n','')});p.view.refresh(true);list.replaceChildren();renderCardList(list,p.plugin);await wait();await frame();
    assert(card.excerptBlocks.map((block:any)=>block.id).join(',')===savedBlocks.map((block:any)=>block.id).join(',')&&card.excerptBlocks.every((block:any,index:number)=>JSON.stringify(block.target)===JSON.stringify(savedBlocks[index].target)),'deleting a block separator lost order or source targets');
    assert(list.querySelectorAll(`[data-card-id="${card.id}"] .ms-card-img[data-excerpt-block] img`).length===2&&p.root.querySelectorAll(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"] img[data-excerpt-block]`).length===2,'deleting a block separator lost images');
    p.store.updateCard(card.id,{front:savedFront,excerptBlocks:savedBlocks});
    for(const marker of ['{{Before image.\nBetween images.}}','{{Before {{nested}} image.\nBetween images.}}']){
      p.store.updateCard(card.id,{front:marker+'\nAfter image.',excerptBlocks:[{...blocks[0],text:marker.split('\n')[0]},blocks[1],{...blocks[2],text:marker.split('\n')[1]},blocks[3],blocks[4]]});
      p.view.refresh(true);list.replaceChildren();renderCardList(list,p.plugin);await wait();await frame();
      const mapText=p.root.querySelector(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"]`)!.textContent||'',listText=list.querySelector(`[data-card-id="${card.id}"]`)!.textContent||'';
      assert(!mapText.includes('Before')&&!mapText.includes('Between')&&!mapText.includes('nested')&&!listText.includes('Before')&&!listText.includes('Between')&&!listText.includes('nested'),'cross-block cloze exposed an answer');
      assert(p.root.querySelectorAll(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"] img[data-excerpt-block]`).length===2&&list.querySelectorAll(`[data-card-id="${card.id}"] .ms-card-img[data-excerpt-block] img`).length===2,'cross-block cloze lost images');
    }
    p.store.updateCard(card.id,{front:savedFront,excerptBlocks:savedBlocks});p.view.refresh(true);list.replaceChildren();renderCardList(list,p.plugin);await wait();await frame();
    const calls:any[]=[];const originalOpen=p.plugin.openDocument;p.plugin.openDocument=async()=>({getDocumentNotebookId:()=>card.documentNotebookId??'default',flashExcerptBlock:(id:string,block:any)=>calls.push([id,block.target])});
    (item.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-source="${blocks[3].id}"]`) as HTMLButtonElement).click();await wait();
    (updated.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-source="${blocks[0].id}"]`) as HTMLButtonElement).click();await wait();
    assert(calls.length===2&&calls[0][0]===card.anchorAnnId&&JSON.stringify(calls[0][1])===JSON.stringify(blocks[3].target)&&JSON.stringify(calls[1][1])===JSON.stringify(blocks[0].target),'block controls did not navigate to their own targets');
    let fallback='';p.plugin.openDocument=async()=>({getDocumentNotebookId:()=>card.documentNotebookId??'default',flashAnnotation:(id:string)=>{fallback=id;}});
    (item.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-source="${blocks[0].id}"]`) as HTMLButtonElement).click();await wait();
    assert(fallback===card.anchorAnnId,'reader without block navigation did not fall back to annotation');
    p.plugin.openDocument=async()=>p.view.getReader();
    (item.querySelector(`[data-excerpt-source="${blocks[3].id}"]`) as HTMLButtonElement).click();await wait(100);
    assert((p.view.getReader() as DocumentReaderView).getBodyEl().dataset.msAnchorNavigation==='exact','real DocumentReader did not navigate to image block');
    p.store.addCard({id:'a11-other',type:'excerpt',docPath:p.gamma.path,anchorAnnId:'a11-other-ann',front:'Other source',tags:[],links:[],createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),excerptBlocks:[{...blocks[0],id:'a11-other-block',text:'Other source'}]});
    p.store.mergeCards(card.id,['a11-other']);list.replaceChildren();renderCardList(list,p.plugin);await wait();
    let mergedPath='',mergedId='',mergedTarget:any;p.plugin.openDocument=async(file:any)=>{mergedPath=file.path;return{getDocumentNotebookId:()=> 'default',flashExcerptBlock:(id:string,block:any)=>{mergedId=id;mergedTarget=block.target;}};};
    (list.querySelector(`[data-card-id="${card.id}"] [data-excerpt-source="a11-other-block"]`) as HTMLButtonElement).click();await wait();
    assert(mergedPath===p.gamma.path&&mergedId==='a11-other-ann'&&JSON.stringify(mergedTarget)===JSON.stringify(blocks[0].target),'merged block opened the wrong source');
    p.store.unmergeCard(card.id);
    p.plugin.openDocument=originalOpen;
    p.store.updateCard(card.id,{front:'Edited excerpt text'});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    assert(card.excerptBlocks?.filter((block:any)=>block.image).length===2&&!card.excerptBlocks.find((block:any)=>block.text)?.target&&p.root.querySelector(`.ms-node[data-id="${node.id}"]`)?.textContent?.includes('Edited excerpt text'),'structural rewrite lost images or retained a stale text target');
    }finally{list.remove();document.documentElement.scrollTop=0;document.body.scrollTop=0;window.scrollTo(0,0);}
  });

  await test('Block condensation requests only missing tiers and display changes stay offline',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),dock=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="MN4 阅读工具栏"]')!,service=aiServiceFor(p.plugin) as any;let calls=0;
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=20;canvas.height=20;p.sources.get(p.alpha.path)!.content='<img width="50" height="50" src="'+canvas.toDataURL()+'">';await p.view.openFile(p.gamma);await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);

    let failNext=false,waitForAbort=false;service.request=async(input:any)=>{calls++;if(failNext)throw new AiError('synthetic','模拟失败');if(waitForAbort)await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>input.signal.addEventListener('abort',()=>reject(new AiError('aborted','已取消浓缩。')),{once:true}));return {text:'synthetic summary',messages:[],drafts:[]};};
    const before=JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)});
    button(dock,'块选择').click();await until(()=>p.root.querySelector('[aria-label^="辅助摘录图片"]'),'block missing');p.root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[aria-label^="辅助摘录图片"]')!.click();
    button(p.root,'块选择：更多').click();button(p.root,'块选择：浓缩').click();const modal=document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-modal')!,output=modal.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('[aria-label="浓缩结果"]')!,level=modal.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>('[aria-label="浓缩档位"]')!,style=modal.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>('[aria-label="浓缩显示样式"]')!;assert(modal.textContent?.includes('synthetic-model')&&modal.textContent?.includes('已绑定')&&calls===0,'service state missing before generation');
    button(modal,'生成浓缩').click();await until(()=>output.value==='synthetic summary','first condensation missing');assert(calls===1&&modal.textContent?.includes('synthetic-model'),'actual service status missing');
    style.value='quote';style.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));assert(output.value==='> synthetic summary'&&calls===1,'display style made a request');
    button(modal,'生成浓缩').click();await until(()=>modal.textContent?.includes('缓存命中'),'cache status missing');assert(calls===1,'cached tier requested provider');
    level.value='points';level.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));button(modal,'生成浓缩').click();await until(()=>calls===2&&modal.textContent?.includes('生成完成'),'new tier was not requested once');
    failNext=true;level.value='outline';level.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));button(modal,'生成浓缩').click();await until(()=>modal.textContent?.includes('模拟失败'),'failure missing');assert(output.value==='','failed tier retained stale result');style.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));assert(output.value===''||output.value==='> ','failed tier restored stale result');
    failNext=false;waitForAbort=true;button(modal,'生成浓缩').click();await until(()=>calls===4,'cancel request missing');button(modal,'取消浓缩').click();await until(()=>modal.textContent?.includes('已取消浓缩'),'cancellation missing');assert(output.value==='','cancellation retained stale result');
    button(modal,'关闭').click();assert(JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)})===before,'condensation changed learning data');
  });

    }finally{(window as any).require=oldRequire;}

    send(page,'pointerdown',203,rect.left+30,rect.top+30);send(window,'pointermove',203,rect.left+130,rect.top+90);send(window,'pointerup',203,rect.left+130,rect.top+90);await until(()=>anns.some(item=>item.type==='region'),'region did not work after cancellation');button(p.root,'移动与选择').click();assert(scroll.style.touchAction==='','leaving region did not restore touch handling');
  });

  });

    assert(node().innerText.includes('A17 question')&&!node().innerText.includes('A17 answer'),'recall did not show the question and cover the answer');
    const toggle=()=>{button(p.root.querySelector('[aria-label="回忆工具"]')!,'回忆设置').click();button(document.querySelector('#test-menu')!,'遮挡卡片正文').click();};
    toggle();await frame();assert(!reader.getRecallSetting('maskCardBody')&&node().innerText.includes('A17 answer'),'body switch did not reveal the answer');
    toggle();await frame();assert(reader.getRecallSetting('maskCardBody')&&!node().innerText.includes('A17 answer')&&JSON.stringify(p.store.getCard('a17-body'))===before,'body switch did not restore mask or changed card content');

  await test('Saved recall settings stay with each study set when switching and reopening documents',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(), first=p.store.activeStudySetId!, reader=()=>p.view.getReader() as any;
    const save=()=>{const dock=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="MN4 阅读工具栏"]')!;if(dock.dataset.mode!=='recall')button(dock,'回忆').click();button(dock,'回忆设置').click();button(document.querySelector('#test-menu')!,'保存回忆设置到当前学习集').click();};
    reader().setRecallSetting('blurExcerpts',true);reader().setRecallSetting('maskCardBody',false);reader().setRecallSetting('maskDocumentExcerpts',false);reader().setRecallCoverAll(false);save();
    const secondSet=p.store.createStudySet('Recall second',[p.alpha.path]);secondSet.scene={templateId:'standard',sceneId:'excerpt',interfaceMode:'traditional'};const second=secondSet.id;p.view.refresh(true);
    assert(!reader().getRecallSetting('blurExcerpts')&&reader().getRecallSetting('maskCardBody')&&reader().getRecallSetting('maskDocumentExcerpts')&&reader().getRecallCoverAll(),'second study set inherited first settings');
    reader().setRecallSetting('singleReveal',true);save();
    p.store.setActiveStudySet(first);p.view.refresh(true);
    assert(reader().getRecallSetting('blurExcerpts')&&!reader().getRecallSetting('maskCardBody')&&!reader().getRecallSetting('maskDocumentExcerpts')&&!reader().getRecallSetting('singleReveal')&&!reader().getRecallCoverAll(),'first study set settings did not return');
    await p.view.openFile(p.beta);await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);
    assert(reader().getRecallSetting('blurExcerpts')&&!reader().getRecallCoverAll(),'settings did not survive reopening reader');
    p.store.setActiveStudySet(second);p.view.refresh(true);
    assert(reader().getRecallSetting('singleReveal')&&!reader().getRecallSetting('blurExcerpts')&&reader().getRecallCoverAll(),'second settings did not return');
    const dock=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="MN4 阅读工具栏"]')!;if(dock.dataset.mode!=='recall')button(dock,'回忆').click();button(dock,'回忆设置').click();button(document.querySelector('#test-menu')!,'恢复默认回忆设置').click();
    assert(!reader().getRecallSetting('singleReveal')&&!p.store.studySets.find((set:any)=>set.id===second)?.recallSettings,'reset did not clear saved study-set override');
    p.store.setActiveStudySet(first);p.view.refresh(true);assert(reader().getRecallSetting('blurExcerpts')&&!reader().getRecallCoverAll(),'reset of second study set changed first set');
  });

  });
  await test('A source card retains independent text when Reader text is added later, edited and removed separately',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();await p.store.saveAnnotationsNow(p.delta.path,[{id:'two-text-source',cardId:'two-text-card',docPath:p.delta.path,docKind:'pdf',page:1,type:'highlight',rects:[{x:20,y:300,w:80,h:18}],pdfCoordinateSpace:'pdf',text:'Source retained',color:'#ffff00',createdAt:'today'}]);
    p.store.addCard({id:'two-text-card',docPath:p.delta.path,type:'excerpt',front:'Source retained',back:'Independent card text',anchorAnnId:'two-text-source',tags:[],links:[],createdAt:'today'});p.store.addNode({id:'two-text-node',cardId:'two-text-card',parentId:null,canvasId:p.store.activeCanvasId,title:'Source retained',collapsed:false,x:40,y:40});
    await p.view.openFile(p.delta);const reader=p.view.getReader() as ReaderView;await reader.renderPage(1);const id=await reader.createLinkedExtend(1,{x:.5,y:.5},'two-text-source');await reader.finishExtendEditing();const card=p.store.getCard('two-text-card')!;
    assert(reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text===''&&card.back==='Independent card text','new source Extend absorbed or erased independent card text');
    await reader.createLinkedExtend(1,{x:.5,y:.5},'two-text-source');const editor=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="留白文字"]');assert(editor,'inline source editor missing');editor.textContent='Later Reader text';editor.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input',{bubbles:true}));await reader.finishExtendEditing();assert(card.back==='Independent card text'&&reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text==='Later Reader text','later Reader text overwrote independent text');
    const open=async()=>{await reader.renderPage(1);await until(()=>p.root.querySelector('[aria-label="留白菜单"]'),'source paper not rendered');button(p.root,'留白菜单').click();button(p.root,'编辑卡片').click();await until(()=>document.querySelector('[aria-label="卡片答案或笔记"]'),'source editor missing');};
    await open();const independent=document.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('[aria-label="卡片答案或笔记"]')!,linked=document.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('[aria-label="来源留白文字"]');assert(linked?.value==='Later Reader text'&&independent.value==='Independent card text','source card has no two editable text blocks: '+JSON.stringify({linked:linked?.value,back:independent.value,extra:card.extendText,notes:reader.getExtendNotes()}));assert(!!(independent.compareDocumentPosition(linked)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING),'later Reader text precedes independent text');linked.value='Card edits Reader text';linked.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));button(document,'保存').click();await until(()=>reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text==='Card edits Reader text','source text edit did not write back');assert(card.back==='Independent card text','source edit changed independent text');
    await p.store.flush();let reopened=new Store(p.store.host);await reopened.load();assert(reopened.getCard(card.id)?.back==='Independent card text'&&reopened.getDocMeta(p.delta.path).pdfExtendNotes?.[0].text==='Card edits Reader text','separate text lost after reload');
    const tile=mapCard(p,p.store.nodes.find(node=>node.cardId===card.id)!.id);await until(()=>tile.textContent?.includes('Independent card text')&&tile.textContent?.includes('Card edits Reader text'),'map omits one text block');
    await open();button(document,'编辑评论').click();button(document,'移除来源留白文字').click();button(document,'取消移除来源文字').click();assert(document.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('[aria-label="来源留白文字"]')!.value==='Card edits Reader text','cancel removed source text');button(document,'移除来源留白文字').click();button(document,'删除来源留白文字').click();assert(reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text==='Card edits Reader text','draft source removal saved early');button(document,'编辑评论').click();button(document,'保存').click();await until(()=>reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text==='','source text removal did not reach Reader');assert(card.back==='Independent card text'&&card.front==='Source retained','source removal lost original or independent text');
    button(p.root,'撤销').click();await until(()=>reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text==='Card edits Reader text','Undo did not restore separate Reader text');assert(card.back==='Independent card text','Undo changed independent text');await until(()=>!button(p.root,'重做').disabled,'Redo unavailable');button(p.root,'重做').click();await until(()=>reader.getExtendNotes()[0].text==='','Redo did not remove Reader text');await p.store.flush();reopened=new Store(p.store.host);await reopened.load();assert(reopened.getCard(card.id)?.back==='Independent card text'&&reopened.getDocMeta(p.delta.path).pdfExtendNotes?.[0].text==='','reloaded removal merged the text blocks');
  });

    const node=p.store.nodes.find(node=>node.cardId===card.id)!,tile=mapCard(p,node.id);await until(()=>tile.querySelector('.ms-node-note')?.textContent==='P01 mixed text remains','map comment missing');const note=tile.querySelector('.ms-node-note')!,images=[...tile.querySelectorAll('img')];assert(images.length===2&&images.every(image=>!!(image.compareDocumentPosition(note)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING)),'source screenshot and Reader handwriting must precede independent text on the card');

  });

  });

  });

    await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);assert(!pane.isConnected&&p.root.dataset.readingTheme==='green','switching to another document kept a stale panel or lost the global study theme');pane=open();assert(button(pane,'Horizontal Flip').disabled&&button(pane,'Vertical Scroll').disabled,'fixed-page flip controls were enabled on flowing HTML');

  });

    assert(!reader.getPageRecord(1)!.wrapper.classList.contains('ms-page-bookmarked'),'bookmark action affected a different page');
    pane=open();assert(![...pane.querySelectorAll('button')].some(row=>row.textContent==='Add Bookmark'),'bookmarked page retained Add Bookmark');button(pane,'Remove Bookmark').click();
    await until(()=>!reader.getPageRecord(2)!.wrapper.classList.contains('ms-page-bookmarked'),'Remove Bookmark did not clear the current page');
    button(open(),'Add Bookmark');assert(p.store.cards.length===2&&p.store.nodes.length===3,'bookmark menu changed learning cards');
  });

      const offset=(top-target.getBoundingClientRect().top)/target.getBoundingClientRect().height;
      button(p.root,'文档目录与书签').click();await wait(100);

    }

  await test('G02 synthetic Ctrl+pixel zero and horizontal-only wheel input do not scale the mindmap',async()=>{
    const {scroll,world,point}=await freshPinchScene(),before=mindmapScale(world);
    ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,0,point.x,point.y,-80);
    const afterHorizontal=mindmapScale(world);
    ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,0,point.x,point.y,0);
    const afterZero=mindmapScale(world);
    assert(Math.abs(afterHorizontal-before)<=1e-6&&Math.abs(afterZero-before)<=1e-6,`horizontal-only and zero Ctrl+pixel input changed scale: initial=${before}, horizontal=${afterHorizontal}, zero=${afterZero}`);
  });
  await test('G03 one light synthetic Ctrl+pixel sample near the upper limit does not immediately hit the boundary',async()=>{
    const {scroll,world,point}=await freshPinchScene();let steps=0;
    while(mindmapScale(world)<2.7&&steps<100){ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,-5,point.x,point.y);steps++;}
    const nearLimit=mindmapScale(world);
    assert(nearLimit>=2.7&&steps<100,`fixture did not reach the upper-limit approach range: scale=${nearLimit}, steps=${steps}`);
    assert(nearLimit<3,`fixture crossed the upper limit before the light sample: ${nearLimit}`);
    ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,-2,point.x,point.y);
    const afterLight=mindmapScale(world);
    assert(afterLight<3,`one light synthetic Ctrl+pixel sample hit the upper scale limit: ${nearLimit} -> ${afterLight}`);
  });
  await test('G04 the same total synthetic Ctrl+pixel delta produces the same scale for one and four events',async()=>{
    const one=await freshPinchScene();
    ctrlPixelWheel(one.scroll,-80,one.point.x,one.point.y);
    const oneEventScale=mindmapScale(one.world);
    const split=await freshPinchScene();
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)ctrlPixelWheel(split.scroll,-20,split.point.x,split.point.y);
    const fourEventScale=mindmapScale(split.world);
    assert(Math.abs(oneEventScale-fourEventScale)<=1e-6,`event splitting changed final scale: one=${oneEventScale}, four=${fourEventScale}`);
  });
  await test('G07 real-log light pinch total follows the reproducible trial curve',async()=>{
    const {scroll,world,point}=await freshPinchScene(),initial=mindmapScale(world),total=-12.914996;
    for(const deltaY of [-2.5,-3.25,-1.414996,-2.75,-3])ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,deltaY,point.x,point.y);
    const actual=mindmapScale(world)/initial,expected=Math.exp(-0.01346909075675091*total);
    assert(Math.abs(actual-expected)<=1e-6,`real-log total did not follow the trial curve: expected=${expected}, actual=${actual}`);
    assert(Math.abs(actual-1.19)<.002,`real-log light pinch moved outside its approximate 1.19 trial target: ${actual}`);
  });
  await test('G08 pixel, line and page wheel units normalize to the same CSS pixel delta',async()=>{
    const pixel=await freshPinchScene();ctrlModeWheel(pixel.scroll,-32,pixel.point.x,pixel.point.y,0);const pixelScale=mindmapScale(pixel.world);
    const line=await freshPinchScene();ctrlModeWheel(line.scroll,-2,line.point.x,line.point.y,1);const lineScale=mindmapScale(line.world);
    const page=await freshPinchScene(),pageHeight=page.scroll.clientHeight;ctrlModeWheel(page.scroll,-32/pageHeight,page.point.x,page.point.y,2);const pageScale=mindmapScale(page.world);
    const expected=Math.exp(0.01346909075675091*32);
    assert(Math.abs(pixelScale-expected)<=1e-5,`pixel wheel did not use its CSS-pixel delta: expected=${expected}, actual=${pixelScale}`);
    assert(Math.abs(lineScale-pixelScale)<=1e-6,`line wheel did not normalize at 16 CSS px per line: pixel=${pixelScale}, line=${lineScale}`);
    assert(Math.abs(pageScale-pixelScale)<=1e-6,`page wheel did not normalize by the current viewport height (${pageHeight} CSS px): pixel=${pixelScale}, page=${pageScale}`);
  });
  await test('G09 background pan follows its pointer id and ends on release, cancel, blur or workspace close',async()=>{
    const tracker=trackWindowPointerListeners();
    const owned=()=>assert(tracker.count('pointermove')===1&&tracker.count('pointerup')===1&&tracker.count('pointercancel')===1&&tracker.count('blur')===1,`background drag did not own all active listeners: ${JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(['pointermove','pointerup','pointercancel','blur'].map(type=>[type,tracker.count(type)])))}`);
    const released=()=>assert(tracker.count()===0,`background drag leaked window listeners: ${tracker.count()}`);
    try{
      const {p,scroll,world,rect,point}=await freshPinchScene(),initialLeft=scroll.scrollLeft,initialTop=scroll.scrollTop;
      pointer(scroll,'pointerdown',point.x,point.y,41);owned();
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+40,point.y+30,99);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft===initialLeft&&scroll.scrollTop===initialTop,'a different pointer id moved the background map');
      pointer(window,'pointerup',point.x+40,point.y+30,99);owned();
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+40,point.y+30,41);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft<initialLeft&&scroll.scrollTop<initialTop,'the owning pointer lost its background drag after another id released');
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+80,point.y+60,41);const movedLeft=scroll.scrollLeft,movedTop=scroll.scrollTop;pointer(window,'pointerup',point.x+80,point.y+60,41);released();
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+120,point.y+90,41);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft===movedLeft&&scroll.scrollTop===movedTop,'pointerup did not stop background movement');

      scroll.scrollLeft=initialLeft;scroll.scrollTop=initialTop;pointer(scroll,'pointerdown',point.x,point.y,42);owned();
      pointer(window,'pointercancel',point.x+40,point.y+30,99);owned();
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+40,point.y+30,42);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft<initialLeft&&scroll.scrollTop<initialTop,'another pointer cancel interrupted the active background drag');
      pointer(window,'pointercancel',point.x+80,point.y+60,42);released();const cancelledLeft=scroll.scrollLeft,cancelledTop=scroll.scrollTop;
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+120,point.y+90,42);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft===cancelledLeft&&scroll.scrollTop===cancelledTop,'pointercancel did not stop background movement');

      scroll.scrollLeft=initialLeft;scroll.scrollTop=initialTop;pointer(scroll,'pointerdown',point.x,point.y,43);owned();
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('blur'));released();const blurredLeft=scroll.scrollLeft,blurredTop=scroll.scrollTop;
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+40,point.y+30,43);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft===blurredLeft&&scroll.scrollTop===blurredTop,'window blur did not stop background movement');

      scroll.scrollLeft=initialLeft;scroll.scrollTop=initialTop;pointer(scroll,'pointerdown',point.x,point.y,44);owned();await closeAll();released();const closedLeft=scroll.scrollLeft,closedTop=scroll.scrollTop;
      pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+40,point.y+30,44);
      assert(scroll.scrollLeft===closedLeft&&scroll.scrollTop===closedTop,'workspace close left background movement active');

      const q=await fresh(),node=mapCard(q,'beta-node'),storedBefore=q.store.getNode('beta-node')!,before={x:storedBefore.x,y:storedBefore.y},box=node.getBoundingClientRect(),nodeScroll=q.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap') as HTMLElement,qWorld=q.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-world') as HTMLElement,nodeLeft=nodeScroll.scrollLeft,nodeTop=nodeScroll.scrollTop,scale=mindmapScale(qWorld);pointer(node,'pointerdown',box.left+10,box.top+10,51);pointer(window,'pointermove',box.left+50,box.top+30,51);pointer(window,'pointerup',box.left+50,box.top+30,51);const after=q.store.getNode('beta-node')!;
      assert(Math.abs(after.x-(before.x+40/scale))<.01&&Math.abs(after.y-(before.y+20/scale))<.01,`background drag changes broke node dragging: before=${JSON.stringify(before)}, after=${JSON.stringify({x:after.x,y:after.y})}, scale=${scale}`);
      assert(nodeScroll.scrollLeft===nodeLeft&&nodeScroll.scrollTop===nodeTop,'node dragging also moved the background map');released();
    }finally{
      for(const pointerId of [41,42,43,44,51])pointer(window,'pointerup',0,0,pointerId);
      tracker.restore();
    }
  });

  await test('node moves update incrementally without rebuilding',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();await wait(200);await p.store.flush();await wait(100);
    const renderer=(p.view as any).renderer,original=renderer.render.bind(renderer);let renders=0,incremental=0;
    renderer.render=()=>{renders++;return original();};
    const update=renderer.updateMovedNodes?.bind(renderer);if(update)renderer.updateMovedNodes=(ids:string[])=>{incremental++;return update(ids);};
    const node=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),before=p.store.getNode('beta-node')!;
    p.store.moveNode(before.id,before.x+30,before.y+20);await wait(120);
    assert(renders===0&&incremental>0&&mapCard(p,'beta-node')===node,'node move rebuilt the graph or skipped incremental update');
  });
  await test('general changes defer graph rebuilding during a gesture',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();await wait(200);await p.store.flush();await wait(100);
    const renderer=(p.view as any).renderer,original=renderer.render.bind(renderer);let renders=0;
    renderer.render=()=>{renders++;return original();};
