  await test('Pad touch drags a card, cancels safely, and pinches without moving it',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),scroll=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap') as HTMLElement,world=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-world') as HTMLElement,node=mapCard(p,'beta-node');
    assert(getComputedStyle(scroll).touchAction==='none','touch gestures are not owned by the map');
    const send=(target:EventTarget,type:string,id:number,x:number,y:number)=>target.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent(type,{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,pointerId:id,pointerType:'touch',button:0,clientX:x,clientY:y}));
    const box=node.getBoundingClientRect(),x=box.left+25,y=box.top+25,before={...p.store.getNode('beta-node')!},scale=mindmapScale(world);
    send(node,'pointerdown',81,x,y);send(window,'pointermove',81,x+50,y+35);assert(node.classList.contains('is-dragging')&&getComputedStyle(node).transitionDuration.split(',').every(v=>parseFloat(v)===0),'card movement is animated instead of following touch');const preview=node.getBoundingClientRect();send(window,'pointerup',81,x+50,y+35);const committed=node.getBoundingClientRect();assert(Math.abs(preview.left-committed.left)<1&&Math.abs(preview.top-committed.top)<1,'card jumped on release');assert(!node.classList.contains('is-dragging'),'drag style remained after release');
    const after=p.store.getNode('beta-node')!;assert(Math.abs(after.x-before.x-50/scale)<.1&&Math.abs(after.y-before.y-35/scale)<.1,'touch did not move card in both axes');
    const first=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),firstBox=first.getBoundingClientRect();send(first,'pointerdown',87,firstBox.left+20,firstBox.top+20);send(window,'pointerup',87,firstBox.left+20,firstBox.top+20);first.click();assert(mapCard(p,'beta-node').classList.contains('is-selected'),'first selection failed');const empty=scroll.getBoundingClientRect();send(scroll,'pointerdown',86,empty.left+8,empty.top+8);send(window,'pointerup',86,empty.left+8,empty.top+8);assert(!mapCard(p,'beta-node').classList.contains('is-selected'),'deselect failed');mapCard(p,'beta-node').click();assert(mapCard(p,'beta-node').classList.contains('is-selected'),'second selection failed');
    const priorY=after.y,twice=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),twiceBox=twice.getBoundingClientRect();send(twice,'pointerdown',85,twiceBox.left+25,twiceBox.top+25);send(window,'pointermove',85,twiceBox.left+25,twiceBox.top+45);p.view.refresh(true);assert(twice.isConnected,'refresh removed a card during the second drag');send(window,'pointermove',85,twiceBox.left+25,twiceBox.top+75);send(window,'pointerup',85,twiceBox.left+25,twiceBox.top+75);assert(Math.abs(p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.y-priorY-50/scale)<.1,'second drag lost its final position after refresh');
    const freshNode=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),b=freshNode.getBoundingClientRect(),saved={x:after.x,y:after.y};send(freshNode,'pointerdown',82,b.left+25,b.top+25);send(window,'pointermove',82,b.left+65,b.top+55);send(window,'pointercancel',82,b.left+65,b.top+55);assert(p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.x===saved.x&&p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.y===saved.y,'cancel committed card movement');
    const r=scroll.getBoundingClientRect(),cx=r.left+r.width/2,cy=r.top+r.height/2,initial=mindmapScale(world);send(scroll,'pointerdown',83,cx-40,cy);send(scroll,'pointerdown',84,cx+40,cy);send(window,'pointermove',84,cx+100,cy);send(window,'pointerup',84,cx+100,cy);send(window,'pointerup',83,cx-40,cy);assert(mindmapScale(world)>initial*1.5,'two touch points did not zoom');assert(p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.x===saved.x&&p.store.getNode('beta-node')!.y===saved.y,'pinch changed card position');
  });
  await test('G10 small maps retain viewport-scaled pan space and keep world coordinates stable at low zoom',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),graph=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-root') as HTMLElement,scroll=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap') as HTMLElement,world=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-world') as HTMLElement;
    p.store.addEdge('parent','beta-node');p.store.updateCanvas(p.canvasId,{handwriting:{version:1,activeLayerId:'default',layers:[{id:'default',name:'Default',strokes:[{id:'g10-ink',tool:'pen',color:'#30343b',width:3,points:[{x:100,y:120},{x:140,y:130}]}]}]}});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const rect=scroll.getBoundingClientRect(),point={x:rect.left+rect.width*.5,y:rect.top+rect.height*.45};
    for(let index=0;index<10;index++)ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,20,point.x,point.y);
    await frame();const scale=mindmapScale(world);assert(Math.abs(scale-.1)<1e-6,'G10 fixture did not reach the minimum zoom');
    const roomX=scroll.scrollWidth-scroll.clientWidth,roomY=scroll.scrollHeight-scroll.clientHeight,multiScreenX=Math.ceil(scroll.clientWidth*2.5),multiScreenY=Math.ceil(scroll.clientHeight*2.5),worldBox=world.getBoundingClientRect();
    assert(roomX>=multiScreenX*2&&roomY>=multiScreenY*2,`low zoom lost multi-screen four-way canvas space: viewport=${scroll.clientWidth}x${scroll.clientHeight}, room=${roomX}x${roomY}, required=${multiScreenX*2}x${multiScreenY*2}`);
    const sideGutters={left:world.offsetLeft,right:scroll.scrollWidth-world.offsetLeft-worldBox.width,top:world.offsetTop,bottom:scroll.scrollHeight-world.offsetTop-worldBox.height},requiredX=scroll.clientWidth*4,requiredY=scroll.clientHeight*4;
    assert(sideGutters.left>=requiredX&&sideGutters.right>=requiredX&&sideGutters.top>=requiredY&&sideGutters.bottom>=requiredY,`low zoom lost four-viewport side gutters: ${JSON.stringify({sideGutters,requiredX,requiredY})}`);
    const ink=()=>p.store.canvases.find(canvas=>canvas.id===p.canvasId)!.handwriting,business=JSON.stringify({nodes:p.store.nodes,edges:p.store.edges,ink:ink()});
    scroll.scrollLeft=Math.round(roomX*.5);scroll.scrollTop=Math.round(roomY*.5);const startLeft=scroll.scrollLeft,startTop=scroll.scrollTop;
    const pan=(pointerId:number,dx:number,dy:number)=>{pointer(scroll,'pointerdown',point.x,point.y,pointerId);pointer(window,'pointermove',point.x+dx,point.y+dy,pointerId);pointer(window,'pointerup',point.x+dx,point.y+dy,pointerId);};
    pan(61,multiScreenX,multiScreenY);assert(scroll.scrollLeft===startLeft-multiScreenX&&scroll.scrollTop===startTop-multiScreenY,'small low-zoom map cannot pan multiple screens toward its top-left content');
    pan(62,-multiScreenX*2,-multiScreenY*2);assert(scroll.scrollLeft===startLeft+multiScreenX&&scroll.scrollTop===startTop+multiScreenY,'small low-zoom map cannot pan multiple screens toward its bottom-right content');
    assert(JSON.stringify({nodes:p.store.nodes,edges:p.store.edges,ink:ink()})===business,'background pan changed node, edge or ink business coordinates');

    const node=mapCard(p,'beta-node'),beforeNode=node.getBoundingClientRect(),beforeScale=mindmapScale(world),zoomX=beforeNode.left+beforeNode.width/2,zoomY=beforeNode.top+beforeNode.height/2;ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,-20,zoomX,zoomY);await frame();
    const afterNode=node.getBoundingClientRect(),anchorError=Math.hypot(afterNode.left+afterNode.width/2-(beforeNode.left+beforeNode.width/2),afterNode.top+afterNode.height/2-(beforeNode.top+beforeNode.height/2));
    assert(mindmapScale(world)>beforeScale&&anchorError<=2,`zooming lost its pointer anchor at low scale: scale ${beforeScale} -> ${mindmapScale(world)}, drift ${anchorError}`);

    const dragScale=mindmapScale(world),stored=p.store.getNode('beta-node')!,before={x:stored.x,y:stored.y},box=node.getBoundingClientRect(),nodeScroll=scroll.scrollLeft,nodeTop=scroll.scrollTop;pointer(node,'pointerdown',box.left+box.width/2,box.top+box.height/2,63);pointer(window,'pointermove',box.left+box.width/2+40,box.top+box.height/2+20,63);pointer(window,'pointerup',box.left+box.width/2+40,box.top+box.height/2+20,63);
    const dragged=p.store.getNode('beta-node')!;
    assert(Math.abs(dragged.x-(before.x+40/dragScale))<.01&&Math.abs(dragged.y-(before.y+20/dragScale))<.01,'node dragging did not preserve world coordinates after zoom and pan');
    assert(scroll.scrollLeft===nodeScroll&&scroll.scrollTop===nodeTop,'node dragging also scrolled the low-zoom map');

    const viewport=scroll.getBoundingClientRect(),worldRect=world.getBoundingClientRect(),createScale=worldRect.width/world.offsetWidth,dblclick=new MouseEvent('dblclick',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,clientX:viewport.left+viewport.width*.62,clientY:viewport.top+viewport.height*.58}),expected={x:(dblclick.clientX-worldRect.left)/createScale,y:(dblclick.clientY-worldRect.top)/createScale},ids=new Set(p.store.nodes.map(item=>item.id));scroll.dispatchEvent(dblclick);const modal=document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-modal');assert(modal,'double-click did not open the new-node editor');const title=modal!.querySelector('textarea')!;title.value='G10 coordinate node';title.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));button(modal!,'确定').click();const created=p.store.nodes.find(item=>!ids.has(item.id))!;assert(created&&Math.abs(created.x-expected.x)<.01&&Math.abs(created.y-expected.y)<.01,`double-click created a node at the wrong world coordinate: expected=${JSON.stringify(expected)}, actual=${JSON.stringify(created&&{x:created.x,y:created.y})}`);

    const priorScroll={left:scroll.scrollLeft,top:scroll.scrollTop},priorInk=JSON.stringify(ink()),priorNodes=JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes);p.store.addNode({id:'g10-far-node',canvasId:p.canvasId,parentId:null,title:'Far recalculation',x:1400,y:900,collapsed:false});p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    assert(scroll.scrollLeft===priorScroll.left&&scroll.scrollTop===priorScroll.top,'render recalculation reset or jumped the user view');
    assert(JSON.stringify(ink())===priorInk&&JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes.slice(0,-1))===priorNodes&&new Set(p.store.nodes.map(item=>item.id)).size===p.store.nodes.length,'render recalculation changed, reordered or duplicated map content or ink');
    assert(graph.querySelector('.ms-node[data-id="g10-far-node"]')&&scroll.scrollWidth-scroll.clientWidth>=scroll.clientWidth*.5,'render recalculation did not extend canvas for far content');
    const resetNodes=JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes),resetInk=JSON.stringify(ink());button(graph,'选择脑图').click();button(document.querySelector('#test-menu')!,'脑图工具与设置…').click();button(document.querySelector('.ms-modal')!,'适应').click();await frame();
    const resetBounds=world.getBoundingClientRect(),resetViewport=scroll.getBoundingClientRect();
    assert(Math.abs(mindmapScale(world)-1)<1e-6&&scroll.scrollLeft>0&&scroll.scrollTop>0,'reset did not restore scale and a two-way inset view');
    assert(resetBounds.left-resetViewport.left>=40&&resetBounds.top-resetViewport.top>=40,'reset returned the map to the top-left corner');
    assert(JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes)===resetNodes&&JSON.stringify(ink())===resetInk&&scroll.scrollWidth-scroll.clientWidth>=scroll.clientWidth*.5,'reset changed business content or discarded pan space');
    document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown',{key:'Escape',bubbles:true,cancelable:true}));
  });
  await test('G05 opposite synthetic Ctrl+pixel input away from both limits restores the initial scale',async()=>{
    const {scroll,world,point}=await freshPinchScene(),initial=mindmapScale(world);
    for(const deltaY of [-24,-24])ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,deltaY,point.x,point.y);
    const magnified=mindmapScale(world);
    assert(magnified>initial&&magnified<3,`forward input did not make an in-range scale change: ${initial} -> ${magnified}`);
    for(const deltaY of [24,24])ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,deltaY,point.x,point.y);
    const restored=mindmapScale(world);
    assert(Math.abs(restored-initial)<=1e-6,`opposite Ctrl+pixel input did not restore scale: ${initial} -> ${magnified} -> ${restored}`);
  });
  await test('G06 off-center synthetic Ctrl+pixel scaling keeps its rendered anchor within 2 CSS px',async()=>{
    const {scroll,world,rect,point}=await freshPinchScene(),initial=mindmapScale(world),worldRect=world.getBoundingClientRect();
    const probe=document.createElement('span');
    const worldX=(point.x-worldRect.left)/initial,worldY=(point.y-worldRect.top)/initial;
    Object.assign(probe.style,{position:'absolute',left:`${worldX}px`,top:`${worldY}px`,width:'1px',height:'1px',opacity:'0',pointerEvents:'none'});
    world.append(probe);await frame();
    const before=probe.getBoundingClientRect();
    ctrlPixelWheel(scroll,-40,point.x,point.y);await frame();
    const after=probe.getBoundingClientRect(),dx=after.left+after.width/2-(before.left+before.width/2),dy=after.top+after.height/2-(before.top+before.height/2),error=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    assert(error<=2,`off-center anchor drifted ${error.toFixed(3)} CSS px (dx=${dx.toFixed(3)}, dy=${dy.toFixed(3)})`);
  });
  await test('Unmodified vertical and horizontal wheel stay with map scrolling and preserve business coordinates',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh(),scroll=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap') as HTMLElement,world=p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-world') as HTMLElement;
    const nodes=JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes),transform=world.style.transform,style=getComputedStyle(scroll);
    assert(style.overflowX==='auto'&&style.overflowY==='auto','map viewport no longer supports normal two-axis scrolling');
    const vertical=new WheelEvent('wheel',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,deltaY:80,clientX:700,clientY:300}),horizontal=new WheelEvent('wheel',{bubbles:true,cancelable:true,deltaX:80,deltaY:0,clientX:700,clientY:300});
    const verticalAccepted=scroll.dispatchEvent(vertical),horizontalAccepted=scroll.dispatchEvent(horizontal);await frame();
    assert(verticalAccepted&&!vertical.defaultPrevented&&horizontalAccepted&&!horizontal.defaultPrevented,'ordinary map wheel was intercepted instead of remaining owned by viewport scrolling');
    assert(world.style.transform===transform,`ordinary map wheel changed scale: ${transform} -> ${world.style.transform}`);
    assert(JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes)===nodes,'ordinary map wheel changed node business coordinates');
  });

  await test('Top Undo follows real document pen input and never falls through to unrelated library history',async()=>{

  });

  await test('Redo of a removed PDF pen cannot overwrite an annotation changed in another view',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh({tabs:[{path:'Delta.pdf'}],activePath:'Delta.pdf'}),reader=p.view.getReader()! as ReaderView;
    await reader.renderPage(1);
    reader.setHandwritingMode('pen');const ink=p.root.querySelector('.ms-workspace-reader .ms-pen-overlay') as HTMLElement,box=ink.getBoundingClientRect();
    pointer(ink,'pointerdown',box.left+40,box.top+50);pointer(ink,'pointermove',box.left+100,box.top+80);pointer(ink,'pointerup',box.left+100,box.top+80);
    const drawn=(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path)).find(ann=>ann.type==='pen')!;assert(drawn?.strokes?.length===1,'PDF pen gesture missing');
    reader.undoLastEdit();assert(!(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path)).some(ann=>ann.id===drawn.id),'PDF creation Undo retained the pen');
    const annotations=await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path),replacement={...drawn,type:'note' as const,text:'Other view has restored this identity',strokes:undefined};
    annotations.push(replacement);await p.store.saveAnnotationsNow(p.delta.path,annotations);const prior=JSON.stringify(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path));
    reader.redoLastEdit();assert(JSON.stringify(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path))===prior&&!reader.getEditHistoryState().canRedo,'PDF pen Redo overwrote or duplicated an annotation restored by another view');
  });
  await test('Undo preserves a new pen used as a primary or secondary card source and keeps its metadata',async()=>{
    for(const link of ['primary','secondary','note'] as const){
      const p=await fresh(),reader=p.view.getReader()!;reader.setHandwritingMode('pen');
      const ink=p.root.querySelector('.ms-workspace-reader .ms-pen-overlay') as HTMLElement,box=ink.getBoundingClientRect();
      pointer(ink,'pointerdown',box.left+40,box.top+50);pointer(ink,'pointermove',box.left+100,box.top+80);pointer(ink,'pointerup',box.left+100,box.top+80);
      const annotations=await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path),drawn=annotations.find(ann=>ann.type==='pen')!,original=JSON.stringify(drawn.strokes);
      if(link==='primary')p.store.updateCard('beta-card',{docPath:p.alpha.path,anchorAnnId:drawn.id});
      else if(link==='secondary')p.store.updateCard('beta-card',{sources:[{docPath:p.alpha.path,anchorAnnId:drawn.id}]});
      else{drawn.noteText='A note added by another view';await p.store.saveAnnotationsNow(p.alpha.path,annotations);}
      const cards=JSON.stringify(p.store.cards);reader.undoLastEdit();
      const empty=(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)).find(ann=>ann.id===drawn.id);
      assert(empty?.strokes?.length===0&&JSON.stringify(p.store.cards)===cards,'Undo removed a linked or edited pen source: '+link);
      if(link==='note')assert(empty.noteText==='A note added by another view','Undo removed another view note');
      reader.redoLastEdit();assert(JSON.stringify((await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)).find(ann=>ann.id===drawn.id)?.strokes)===original,'Redo lost linked pen data: '+link);
      await closeAll();
    }
  });

  await test('Switching document tabs and closing the workspace release handwriting subscriptions',async()=>{
    const counts=new Map<any,{mode:number;preference:number}>(),restore:(()=>void)[]=[];
    for(const Reader of [DocumentReaderView,ReaderView,WordReaderView])for(const [name,key] of [['subscribeHandwritingModeChange','mode'],['subscribeHandwritingSyncPreference','preference']] as const){const prototype=Reader.prototype as any,original=prototype[name];prototype[name]=function(callback:any){const record=counts.get(this)??{mode:0,preference:0};counts.set(this,record);record[key]++;let closed=false;const release=original.call(this,callback);return()=>{if(!closed){closed=true;record[key]--;release();}};};restore.push(()=>{prototype[name]=original;});}
    try{
      const p=await fresh(),first=p.view.getReader()!;assert(counts.get(first)?.mode===1&&counts.get(first)?.preference===1,'first reader was not connected exactly once');first.setHandwritingSyncPreference(true);first.setHandwritingMode('highlight');
      await p.view.openFile(p.delta);const second=p.view.getReader()!;assert(counts.get(first)?.mode===0&&counts.get(first)?.preference===0&&counts.get(second)?.mode===1&&counts.get(second)?.preference===1,'tab switch retained old handwriting callbacks');
      second.setHandwritingSyncPreference(true);second.setHandwritingMode('eraser');assert(mapInk(p).dataset.inkMode==='eraser','PDF reader did not join mode sync');await p.view.openFile(p.gamma);const third=p.view.getReader()!;assert(counts.get(second)?.mode===0&&counts.get(second)?.preference===0&&counts.get(third)?.mode===1,'PDF→Word switch retained callbacks');
      third.setHandwritingSyncPreference(true);third.setHandwritingMode('lasso');assert(mapInk(p).dataset.inkMode==='lasso','Word reader did not join mode sync');await p.view.onClose();p.view.unload();assert([...counts.values()].every(value=>value.mode===0&&value.preference===0),'workspace close leaked a mode or preference subscription');active.splice(active.indexOf(p),1);p.view.containerEl.remove();await p.store.flush();
    }finally{restore.reverse().forEach(action=>action());}
  });

  await test('Entering and leaving focus mode preserves PDF, HTML and Word reading positions',async()=>{
    for(const format of ['pdf','html','word']){
      await closeAll();const p=await fresh();p.view.containerEl.style.width='950px';
      if(format==='pdf'){
        p.sources.get(p.delta.path)!.binary=new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({pages:8,rotations:[0,90]})).buffer;await p.view.openFile(p.delta);
      }else if(format==='word')await p.view.openFile(p.gamma);
      const reader=p.view.getReader()!,scroll=reader.getScrollContainer();
      const wanted=format==='pdf'?{scale:1.138,pdfFitWidth:false,position:{page:2,pageOffset:.12,left:0}}:{position:{ratio:.54}};
      await reader.setState({...reader.getState(),...wanted});await frame();
      const before=reader.getState() as any;
      const restored=()=>{
        if(reader instanceof ReaderView){
          const page=reader.getPageRecord(before.position.page)!.wrapper.getBoundingClientRect();
          return reader.getScale()===before.scale&&Math.abs((scroll.getBoundingClientRect().top-page.top)/page.height-before.position.pageOffset)<.02;
        }
        if(before.position.block!==undefined){
          const blocks=[...scroll.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('p,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,li,pre,blockquote')].filter(node=>!node.closest('[data-ann-id],.ms-free-overlay')&&node.getBoundingClientRect().height>0),block=blocks[before.position.block];
          return !!block&&Math.abs((scroll.getBoundingClientRect().top-block.getBoundingClientRect().top)/block.getBoundingClientRect().height-before.position.blockOffset)<.08;
        }
        const section=[...scroll.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-ms-section]')].find(node=>node.dataset.msSection===before.position.section);
        if(section&&Number.isFinite(before.position.sectionOffset)){
          const bounds=section.getBoundingClientRect();return Math.abs((scroll.getBoundingClientRect().top-bounds.top)/bounds.height-before.position.sectionOffset)<.03;
        }
        return Math.abs(scroll.scrollTop/Math.max(1,scroll.scrollHeight-scroll.clientHeight)-before.position.ratio)<.03;
      };
      assert(restored(),format+' fixture did not establish a reading position');
      button(p.root,'专注工作区').click();await until(restored,format+' focus mode lost the actual reading viewport');
      document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown',{key:'Escape',bubbles:true,cancelable:true}));
      await until(restored,format+' leaving focus mode lost the actual reading viewport');
      assert(p.view.getReader()===reader,'focus transition replaced the reader');
      button(p.root,'专注工作区').click();await until(restored,format+' repeated focus transition lost the viewport');
      const focused=reader.getState() as any;await closeAll();
      const saved=p.store.getWorkspaceState() as any,tab=saved.tabs.find((tab:any)=>tab.path===saved.activePath);
      assert(JSON.stringify(tab.state.position)===JSON.stringify(focused.position),format+' closing a focused workspace saved the detached viewport');
    }
  });

    const name=editor.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[aria-label="模板名称"]')!;name.value='合成模板';name.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));
    const tool=editor.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[data-template-tool="excerpt.rectangle"]')!;assert(tool.checked,'source tool missing');tool.click();
    const palette=editor.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[aria-label="摘录颜色"]')!;palette.value='#123456, #abcdef';
    const popup=editor.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[aria-label="摘录后显示浮窗"]')!;popup.checked=true;
    const menu=editor.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[aria-label="选区菜单：朗读"]')!;menu.click();
    const penColor=editor.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[aria-label="笔颜色 pen-1"]')!;penColor.value='#112233';
    button(editor,'保存模板').click();await frame();
    const saved=p.store.getSceneTemplates().find((item:any)=>item.name==='合成模板');assert(saved&&!saved.scenes[0].tools.includes('excerpt.rectangle'),'tool change not saved');
    assert(JSON.stringify(p.store.getSceneTemplates().find((item:any)=>item.id==='standard'))===JSON.stringify(before),'source template changed');
    p.store.selectStudyScene(p.set.id,saved.id,'excerpt');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    assert(!p.root.querySelector('.ms-bottom-scene-tools [data-ms-tool="excerpt.rectangle"]'),'disabled template tool remained in scene');
    assert(p.root.querySelector('.ms-bottom-scene-tools [data-ms-tool="excerpt.text"]'),'enabled template tool disappeared');
    const applied=p.store.getEffectiveSceneSettings();assert(applied.colors.join('|')==='#123456|#abcdef'&&applied.excerptPopup===true&&applied.textSelectionMenuActions.includes('speech')&&applied.handwritingPresets[0].color==='#112233','saved template settings did not become effective');
    assert(p.store.settings.colors.join('|')!==applied.colors.join('|'),'template settings overwrote global preferences');
    await p.store.flush();const reopened=new Store(p.store.host);await reopened.load();assert(JSON.stringify(reopened.getSceneTemplates().find((item:any)=>item.id===saved.id))===JSON.stringify(saved),'edited template did not survive reopen');

  await test('R-C3 excerpt pens retain independent continuous state without business writes',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();p.store.setStudyInterface(p.set.id,'scenes');await p.view.openFile(p.delta);p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const bar=()=>p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="学习场景工具栏"]')!;
    const tool=(id:string)=>bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-ms-tool="${id}"]`)!;
    const click=async(id:string)=>{tool(id).click();await frame();};
    const before=JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path)});
    await click('excerpt.text');assert(tool('excerpt.text').getAttribute('aria-current')==='true'&&tool('excerpt.rectangle').getAttribute('aria-current')==='false','R-C3a: pen identity/current state');
    await click('continuous-excerpt');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true','R-C3c: text must be on');
    await click('excerpt.rectangle');assert(tool('excerpt.rectangle').getAttribute('aria-current')==='true'&&tool('excerpt.text').getAttribute('aria-current')==='false','R-C3a: rectangle current');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','R-C3b: rectangle inherited text state');
    await click('excerpt.text');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true','R-C3b: text lost state');
    await click('continuous-excerpt');await click('excerpt.rectangle');await click('continuous-excerpt');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true','R-C3b: rectangle did not activate');
    await click('excerpt.text');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','R-C3b: text inherited rectangle state');
    await click('excerpt.rectangle');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true','R-C3b: rectangle lost state');await click('continuous-excerpt');
    p.store.selectStudyScene(p.set.id,'read-write-recall','writing');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    await click('excerpt.text');await click('continuous-excerpt');await click('excerpt.rectangle');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false'&&tool('excerpt.rectangle').getAttribute('aria-current')==='true','R-C3: writing rectangle inherited text state');
    await click('excerpt.text');assert(tool('continuous-excerpt').getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true','R-C3: writing text state lost');await click('continuous-excerpt');
    assert(JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.delta.path)})===before,'R-C3: business data changed');
  });

    const select=(id:string)=>{tabs.find(tab=>tab.dataset.sceneId===id)!.click();};
    select('handwriting');await frame();
    const row=bar.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools:not([aria-hidden="true"])')!;
    const pens=()=>[...bar.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools:not([aria-hidden="true"])')!.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-ms-tool^="pen."]')];
    assert(pens().map(button=>button.dataset.msTool).join('|')==='pen.1|pen.2','normal pens lack distinct stable tool IDs');
    assert(row.querySelector('[data-ms-tool="lasso"]')?.getAttribute('aria-label')==='套索','lasso stable ID missing');
    assert(row.querySelector('[data-ms-tool="eraser"]')?.getAttribute('aria-label')==='橡皮擦','eraser stable ID missing');
    const business=JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)});
    const activePenCount=()=>pens().filter(button=>button.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true').length;
    assert(activePenCount()<=1,'initial state marked both normal pens active');
    pens()[0].click();await frame();assert(activePenCount()===1&&pens()[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'&&pens()[1].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','selecting pen 1 did not exclusively activate pen 1');
    pens()[1].click();await frame();assert(activePenCount()===1&&pens()[1].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'&&pens()[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','selecting pen 2 did not exclusively activate pen 2');
    assert(JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)})===business,'switching pen presets created ink or changed business data');
    select('recall');await frame();const recallReader=p.view.getReader() as ReaderView;recallReader.setRecallMode(true);p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const recallRow=bar.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools:not([aria-hidden="true"])')!;
    recallRow.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-ms-tool="ink"]')!.click();await frame();
    assert(p.store.getStudyScene(p.set.id)?.sceneId==='recall'&&recallReader.getRecallMode(),'recall handwriting exited recall instead of overlaying the handwriting tool');
    assert(JSON.stringify({cards:p.store.cards,nodes:p.store.nodes,annotations:await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)})===business,'entering recall handwriting changed business data');
    select('organize');await frame();const organizeRow=bar.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools:not([aria-hidden="true"])')!;
    const copyMove=organizeRow.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-ms-tool="copy-move"]')!,links=organizeRow.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-ms-tool="links"]')!;
    copyMove.click();await frame();assert(document.querySelector('#test-menu [role="menuitem"]'),'copy/move menu did not open from its scene tool');document.querySelector('#test-menu')?.remove();
    links.click();await frame();assert(p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-root')?.classList.contains('is-connect-mode'),'scene link tool did not enter connect mode');links.click();await frame();assert(!p.root.querySelector('.ms-mindmap-root')?.classList.contains('is-connect-mode'),'scene link tool did not exit connect mode');
    assert(!organizeRow.querySelector('[data-ms-tool="selection"]'),'organize row retained multi-select');
  });
  await test('A04 R1-R7: ordinary pen pressed state stays exclusive through tool changes and collapse with zero ink writes',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();p.store.setStudyInterface(p.set.id,'scenes');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const bar=()=>p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="学习场景工具栏"]')!;
    bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tab[data-scene-id="handwriting"]')!.click();await frame();
    const row=()=>bar().querySelector<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools[data-scene-id="handwriting"]')!;
    const pens=()=>[...row().querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-ms-tool^="pen."]')];
    const countPressed=()=>pens().filter(pen=>pen.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true').length;
    const assertR1=()=>assert(countPressed()===0||countPressed()===1,'R1: ordinary pen buttons may never both be aria-pressed=true');
    const clickTool=async(id:string)=>{const target=row().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-ms-tool="${id}"]`);assert(target,`R1-R7: missing ${id} tool`);target!.click();await frame();assertR1();};
    const beforeAnnotations=JSON.stringify(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path));
    const reader=p.view.getReader() as ReaderView;
    assertR1();
    await clickTool('pen.1');
    assert(pens()[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'&&pens()[1].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','R2: selecting ordinary pen 1 must mark only pen 1 pressed');
    await clickTool('pen.2');
    assert(pens()[1].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'&&pens()[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','R3: selecting ordinary pen 2 must mark only pen 2 pressed');
    await clickTool('pen.1');await clickTool('highlighter');
    assert(pens().every(pen=>pen.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false')&&p.root.dataset.readerTool==='highlight','R4: highlighter must clear both ordinary pen states and select highlight mode');
    await clickTool('pen.2');await clickTool('hand');
    assert(pens().every(pen=>pen.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false')&&p.root.dataset.readerTool==='hand','R5: hand tool must clear both ordinary pen states and select hand mode');
    await clickTool('pen.2');
    bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-collapse')!.click();await frame();assertR1();
    const collapsed=bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-collapse')!;
    assert(collapsed.getAttribute('aria-expanded')==='false','R6: capsule collapse control must report aria-expanded=false');
    const selectedIndex=pens().findIndex(pen=>pen.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true');
    const normalPresetIds=(p.store.settings.handwritingPresets??[]).filter(preset=>preset.type!=='pencil'&&preset.type!=='highlighter').map(preset=>preset.id);
    assert(selectedIndex>=0&&reader.getActiveHandwritingPreset?.()===normalPresetIds[selectedIndex],'R6: collapsed capsule left a stale pen state or preset/button identity mismatch');
    const afterAnnotations=JSON.stringify(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path));
    assert(afterAnnotations===beforeAnnotations,'R7: tool changes must leave annotation fields exactly unchanged');
    assert(!(JSON.parse(afterAnnotations) as any[]).some(item=>item.type==='ink'),'R7: tool changes must not create ink annotations');
  });
  await test('reader auto-review toggle persists and enrolls only newly created cards without clearing existing membership',async()=>{
    const p=await fresh();assert(p.store.settings.autoReview===false,'auto-review must default off');
    p.store.setStudyInterface(p.set.id,'scenes');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const bar=()=>p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="学习场景工具栏"]')!;
    const selectRecall=()=>bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tab[data-scene-id="recall"]')!.click();
    selectRecall();await frame();
    const autoReview=()=>bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-ms-tool="auto-review"]')!;
    assert(autoReview().getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','auto-review tool did not expose its initial off state');
    autoReview().click();await frame();
    assert(autoReview().getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'&&p.store.settings.autoReview===true,'enabling auto-review did not persist an active setting');
    await p.store.flush();const enabledReopened=new Store(p.store.host);await enabledReopened.load();
    assert(enabledReopened.settings.autoReview===true,'auto-review on state did not survive Store reopen');
    assert(!!document.querySelector('.ms-deck-modal'),'enabling without a current deck did not open the existing deck chooser');
    button(document.querySelector('.ms-deck-modal')!,'关闭').click();await frame();
    bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tab[data-scene-id="excerpt"]')!.click();await frame();
    const before=p.store.cards.length;annotation(p).click();await frame();button(document,'摘录卡').click();await frame();
    assert(p.store.cards.length===before+1,'reader card creation failed without a review deck');
    assert(p.store.reviewDecks.length===0,'auto-review created a review deck implicitly');
    assert(notices.some((notice:any)=>String(notice?.text??notice).includes('未加入复习')),'missing-deck creation did not explain that the card was not enrolled');
    const unqueued=p.store.cards.at(-1)!;
    const deck=p.store.createReviewDeck('Auto review',p.set.id);await p.store.flush();
    annotation(p).click();await frame();button(document,'摘录卡').click();await frame();const enrolled=p.store.cards.at(-1)!;
    assert(deck.cardIds.includes(enrolled.id),'new reader card was not added to the current review deck');
    selectRecall();await frame();autoReview().click();await frame();assert(autoReview().getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='false','disabling auto-review did not update aria-pressed');
    assert(deck.cardIds.includes(enrolled.id),'disabling auto-review removed an existing review deck member');
    bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tab[data-scene-id="excerpt"]')!.click();await frame();annotation(p).click();await frame();button(document,'摘录卡').click();await frame();const afterOff=p.store.cards.at(-1)!;
    assert(!deck.cardIds.includes(afterOff.id),'new reader card entered the deck after auto-review was disabled');
    assert(!deck.cardIds.includes(unqueued.id),'turning auto-review on later backfilled an earlier card');
    await p.store.flush();const reopened=new Store(p.store.host);await reopened.load();
    assert(reopened.settings.autoReview===false,'auto-review off state did not survive Store reopen');
    assert(reopened.getReviewDeck(deck.id)?.cardIds.includes(enrolled.id),'existing review membership did not survive Store reopen');
  });
  // --- candidate seam: A04 scene-tool id/action probe (additive, no existing test changed) ---
  (window as any).sceneToolsProbe=async()=>{
    await closeAll();
    const p=await fresh();
    p.store.setStudyInterface(p.set.id,'scenes');
    p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    await p.view.openFile(p.alpha);p.view.refresh(true);await frame();
    const view:any=p.view;
    const bar=()=>{const node=p.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-label="学习场景工具栏"]');if(!node)throw new Error('scene bar missing');return node;};
    const activeRow=()=>{const rows=[...bar().querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools')].filter(row=>row.getAttribute('aria-hidden')!=='true');if(rows.length!==1)throw new Error('active tools rows: '+rows.length);return rows[0];};
    const read=async()=>({
      scene:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.store.getStudyScene(p.set.id))),
      tabs:[...bar().querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tab')].map(tab=>({sceneId:tab.dataset.sceneId,label:tab.getAttribute('aria-label'),role:tab.getAttribute('role'),selected:tab.getAttribute('aria-selected'),pressed:tab.getAttribute('aria-pressed')})),
      activeRows:bar().querySelectorAll('.ms-bottom-scene-tools[aria-hidden="false"], .ms-bottom-scene-tools:not([aria-hidden])').length,
      tools:[...activeRow().querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-action')].map((item,index)=>({index,label:item.getAttribute('aria-label'),toolId:item.dataset.msTool??null,icon:item.dataset.icon??null,disabled:item.disabled,pressed:item.getAttribute('aria-pressed'),title:item.title})),
      collapse:{label:bar().querySelector('.ms-bottom-collapse')?.getAttribute('aria-label')??null,expanded:bar().querySelector('.ms-bottom-collapse')?.getAttribute('aria-expanded')??null},
      readerTool:view.readerTool,
      titleLinks:view.reader?.getTitleLinksEnabled?.()??null,
      blockSelection:view.reader?.getBlockSelectionMode?.()??null,
      handwriting:view.reader?.getHandwritingMode?.()??null,
      preset:view.reader?.getActiveHandwritingPreset?.()??null,
      aiHint:view.reader?.getRecallAiHint?.()??null,
      popup:view.popup?{className:String(view.popup.className),label:view.popup.getAttribute('aria-label')}:null,
      menu:[...document.querySelectorAll('#test-menu [role="menuitem"], #test-menu')].map(node=>node.textContent),
      cards:JSON.stringify(p.store.cards),nodes:JSON.stringify(p.store.nodes),
      annotations:JSON.stringify(await p.store.loadAnnotations(p.alpha.path)),
      notices:[...document.querySelectorAll('.ms-workspace-notice')].map(node=>node.textContent),
      capsuleCount:p.root.querySelectorAll('.ms-bottom-capsule-shell').length,
    });
    const select=async(sceneId:string)=>{const tab=[...bar().querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tab')].find(item=>item.dataset.sceneId===sceneId);if(!tab)throw new Error('missing scene tab '+sceneId);tab.click();await frame();await wait(30);};
    const click=async(label:string)=>{const item=[...activeRow().querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-action')].find(node=>node.getAttribute('aria-label')===label);if(!item)return{clicked:false,reason:'missing tool '+label};if(item.disabled)return{clicked:false,reason:'disabled tool '+label};const before=await read();item.click();await frame();await wait(60);const after=await read();if(view.popup)view.dismissPopup();document.querySelector('#test-menu')?.remove();await frame();return{clicked:true,before,after};};
    const snapshots:any={};
    for(const sceneId of ['excerpt','handwriting','organize','recall']){await select(sceneId);snapshots[sceneId]=await read();}
    await select('excerpt');
    const actions:any={};
    actions['手型（摘录笔盒）']=await click('手型（摘录笔盒）');
    actions['文本']=await click('文本');
    actions['矩形']=await click('矩形');
    actions['更多']=await click('更多');
    actions['标题链接']=await click('标题链接');
    actions['标题链接.restore']=await click('标题链接');
    actions['块选择']=await click('块选择');
    actions['块选择.restore']=await click('块选择');
    await select('handwriting');
    for(const label of ['普通笔 1','普通笔 2','荧光笔','套索','橡皮擦','手形工具'])actions['handwriting:'+label]=await click(label);
    await select('recall');
    actions['recall:AI提示']=await click('AI提示');
    actions['recall:AI提示.restore']=await click('AI提示');
    actions['recall:回忆设置']=await click('回忆设置');
    actions['recall:重置']=await click('重置');
    await select('organize');
    actions['organize:层级']=await click('层级');
    const organizeUntouched=(()=>{const row=activeRow();return [...row.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-action')].map(item=>({label:item.getAttribute('aria-label'),disabled:item.disabled}));})();
    await select('excerpt');
    const restored=await read();
    // --- candidate seam (additive): read-write-recall template rows for A04 reading/writing ---
    const templateSnapshots:any={};
    const allRows=()=>[...bar().querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools')].map(row=>({
      sceneId:row.getAttribute('data-scene-id'),hidden:row.getAttribute('aria-hidden'),
      labels:[...row.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-action')].map(item=>item.getAttribute('aria-label')),
      capabilities:[...row.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-capability')].map(node=>node.textContent),
      toolIds:[...row.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-action')].map(item=>(item.dataset.msTool??null))}));
    const readLite=async()=>{
      const collapseNode=bar().querySelector('.ms-bottom-collapse');
      const rowNodes=[...bar().querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-scene-tools')];
      return {
        collapsed:collapseNode?.getAttribute('aria-expanded')??null,
        rows:rowNodes.map(row=>{
          const visible=[...row.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.ms-bottom-action')].filter(item=>item.offsetParent!==null);
          return {sceneId:row.getAttribute('data-scene-id'),hidden:row.getAttribute('aria-hidden'),
                  visibleLabels:visible.map(item=>item.getAttribute('aria-label'))};
        }),
        capsuleCount:p.root.querySelectorAll('.ms-bottom-capsule-shell').length};
    };
    for(const sceneId of ['reading','writing']){
      p.store.selectStudyScene(p.set.id,'read-write-recall',sceneId as any);p.view.refresh(true);await frame();await wait(40);
      const full:any=await read();
      full.allRows=allRows();
      const collapseBtn=bar().querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.ms-bottom-collapse');
      collapseBtn?.click();await frame();await wait(40);
      full.collapsedState=await readLite();
      collapseBtn?.click();await frame();await wait(40);
      full.reExpandedState=await readLite();
      templateSnapshots['read-write-recall:'+sceneId]=full;
    }
    (window as any).templateRowDetail={allRows:allRows(),...{}};
    p.store.selectStudyScene(p.set.id,'standard','excerpt');p.view.refresh(true);await frame();await wait(40);
    const restoredTemplate=await read();
    return {snapshots,actions,organizeUntouched,restored,templateSnapshots,restoredTemplate,notices:[...(window as any).notices??[]]};
  };
}catch(error){(window as any).fatal=String(error);}finally{(window as any).done=true;}
